We achieve this through a distribution-aware trajectory sampling mechanism, which selects trajectories from a redundant exploration space for each prompt, and an adaptive redundancy allocation scheme to maximize both shaping effectiveness and system efficiency. Experiments demonstrate significant acceleration over state-of-the-art systems by up to 1.77x without compromising model performance.

</details>


### [149] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了逆强化学习（IRL）与结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）模型在数学上等价，系统梳理了两大社区从经典识别方法到现代机器学习方法的演进脉络，并指出了各方法的局限性与识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将两个独立发展的领域——机器学习的IRL和计量经济学的DDC——统一起来，厘清它们实际上在研究同一个概率模型但使用了不同的术语和方法论，从而促进跨领域交流与方法融合。

Method: 首先证明IRL与DDC的等价性；然后梳理经典识别结果（Magnac & Thesmar）及四种计算范式（Rust嵌套不动点、Hotz-Miller条件选择概率、Adusumilli & Eckardt的两种时序差分方法）；接着分析现代ML/IRL方法（对抗IRL、占用匹配、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL）；最后介绍Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架。

Result: 明确了各方法的实际优化目标及其能识别什么、不能识别什么；指出了各方法的固有局限：维度灾难、转移核估计困难、"致命三要素"问题、投影不动点偏差等。

Conclusion: 离线IRL/DDC问题可通过经验风险最小化框架下的梯度估计器有效求解，但不同方法在识别能力上存在本质差异，需要根据具体场景选择合适的估计策略。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [150] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo,Hoyoung Kim,Minwoo Jang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedVPA-GP框架，通过联邦混合先验和正交损失解决了联邦学习中变分偏好对齐的后验坍塌问题，实现了用户偏好的解耦与个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习框架使用单一奖励模型，平均化了用户间冲突的偏好（如有益性vs无害性）；变分偏好学习在去中心化场景下因本地数据稀缺和异质性导致后验坍塌。

Method: 提出联邦变分偏好对齐框架FedVPA-GP，包含：1) 联邦混合先验（Federated Mixture Prior），使客户端利用全局分布作为动态先验；2) 正交损失（Orthogonal Loss），在潜在空间中显式分离偏好原型；3) Gumbel-Softmax先验用于离散偏好建模。

Result: 在HH-RLHF数据集上，FedVPA-GP显著优于单一奖励模型基线，成功解耦了冲突的用户意图，并实现了动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP在保护隐私的前提下有效解耦了异构用户偏好，为联邦环境下的个性化LLM对齐提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


### [151] [ForecastCompass: Guiding Agentic Forecasting with Adaptive Factor Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30858)
*Yurui Chang,Yongkang Du,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ForecastCompass (FoCo)，一种基于自适应因子记忆的智能预测框架，通过分层预测任务分类法、因子记忆和推理记忆两个互补组件，以及迭代记忆修订机制，提升智能体的概率预测准确性和校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法不适用于预测任务，通常存储过去交互、反思或事实关联，缺乏对可复用预测因子或校准知识的显式表示，导致无法有效利用历史预测经验。

Method: FoCo包含：1) 分层预测任务分类法组织预测经验；2) 因子记忆（捕获可复用预测维度）和推理记忆（编码概率更新、不确定性处理和校准原则）两个互补组件；3) 通过回顾性分析作为学习信号，采用口头化记忆修订程序迭代更新记忆。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX数据集上，使用GPT-5-mini和Gemini-2.5-Flash进行实验，FoCo在概率准确性和校准性方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: 基于因子的自适应记忆框架能有效积累可迁移的预测知识，提升智能体在动态环境中的预测表现。

Abstract: Agentic forecasting is important for decision-making in dynamic environments, but it remains challenging because agents must reason from incomplete, time-limited evidence and produce calibrated probabilities before outcomes are resolved. Memory provides a natural mechanism for transferring experience from resolved forecasts to future prediction tasks. However, existing agent-memory methods are not tailored to forecasting, as they typically store past interactions, reflections, or factual associations without explicitly representing reusable predictive factors or calibration knowledge. We propose ForecastCompass (FoCo), an adaptive factor-based memory framework for agentic forecasting. FoCo organizes forecasting experience with a hierarchical forecasting-task taxonomy, enabling retrieval task-relevant forecasting knowledge. It maintains two complementary memory components: factor memory, which captures reusable predictive dimensions, and reasoning memory, which encodes probability updating, uncertainty handling, and calibration principles. Using retrospective analyses as learning signals, FoCo iteratively revises memory through a verbalized memory-revision procedure, enabling the agent to accumulate transferable forecasting knowledge over time. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX with GPT-5-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash show that FoCo improves both probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [152] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning without an Optimal Demonstrator: A Feasible Reward Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30903)
*Kihyun Kim,Shripad Deshmukh,Nikos Vlassis,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可行奖励集框架，用于从多个具有异质性次优水平的非最优演示者中学习奖励函数，通过为每个演示者编码声明的次优水平作为线性约束，并取可行集的交集，理论分析了可行集的单调收缩性及严格收紧条件，给出了真实最优奖励的恢复保证，并设计了处理奖励模糊性的策略及适用于高维环境的离线算法，在网格世界和大语言模型微调任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习通常假设演示来自单一最优演示者，但实际应用中数据往往来自多个具有异质性次优水平的非完美演示者，现有方法难以处理这种设置下的奖励学习问题。

Method: 提出可行奖励集框架：为每个演示者编码其声明的次优水平作为线性约束，取可行集的交集；理论分析可行集的单调收缩性与严格收紧条件；推导真实最优奖励的两个恢复保证；引入处理奖励模糊性的策略；提出适用于高维环境的离线函数逼近算法。

Result: 理论证明联合可行集随数据添加单调收缩，并精确刻画了新演示者严格收紧可行集的条件；建立了基于最优占优接近度和充分覆盖条件的两类恢复保证；在表格型网格世界和大语言模型微调实验中，结果与理论预测一致，且所提框架优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效处理了多异质次优演示者的奖励学习问题，理论分析与实验结果验证了其可行性与优势，为实际应用中从非完美演示者学习奖励提供了可靠方法。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) typically assumes demonstrations from a single optimal demonstrator, but in many applications data come from multiple imperfect demonstrators with heterogeneous suboptimality levels. We study reward learning in this setting through a feasible-reward-set framework: for each demonstrator, we encode its declared suboptimality level as a linear constraint and intersect the resulting feasible sets across demonstrators. Our theoretical analysis shows that the joint feasible set shrinks monotonically as data are added, and we give an exact characterization of when a new demonstrator strictly tightens it. We further establish two recovery guarantees for the feasible reward set of the ground-truth optimal demonstrator: one bound depends on closeness to the optimal occupancy, while the other requires only sufficient coverage and no near-optimal demonstrator. On the practical side, we introduce strategies to address the inherent reward ambiguity in the obtained reward set and provide an offline algorithm with function approximation for high-dimensional environments. Experiments in tabular grid-world and large language model (LLM) fine-tuning settings are consistent with the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework over baselines.

</details>


### [153] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM是一种轻量级CGM基础模型，通过将血糖信号分解为慢速生理状态流和快速事件流，在七个临床预测任务中平均PR-AUC提升4.1个点，优于现有CGM基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列和CGM基础模型将血糖轨迹编码为单一纠缠序列，未能显式建模血糖动态中不同时间尺度的结构信息，限制了表征的可迁移性。

Method: 将不规则CGM记录对齐到24小时时间网格并保留观测掩码；将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬态事件流；采用两种预训练目标：掩码上下文潜在预测和时态动态预测。

Result: 在四个数据集、七个任务上取得最优线性探测性能，平均PR-AUC提升4.1点；在糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务上全面领先，在3/4的胰岛素抵抗任务上最优；跨数据集迁移和少样本学习表现最佳。

Conclusion: 生理感知的状态-事件分解是一种有效的归纳偏置，能够提升CGM表示学习的可迁移性。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [154] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DareU，首个基于数据归因奖励的LLM遗忘框架，通过强化学习降低生成响应与遗忘数据之间的归因分数，在保持模型效用的同时实现有效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法多依赖优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集损失），存在过度遗忘和模型效用差等关键问题。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出更平衡的遗忘方法。

Method: 将LLM遗忘的优化目标重新定义为归因归零，提出DareU框架，采用强化学习通过降低生成响应对遗忘数据所有者的归因分数（即去归因）来更新LLM。使用LLM分类器作为归因的高效近似。

Result: 实验结果表明DareU优于现有基线方法，在实现有效遗忘的同时，很好地平衡了遗忘质量与模型效用。

Conclusion: 基于数据归因奖励的强化学习框架为LLM遗忘提供了新思路，有效缓解了过度遗忘问题，为未来遗忘方法研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [155] [Zero Collapse: A Failure Mode of Policy Gradient Methods in Discontinuous Reward Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30896)
*Nishant Kumar,Enrique Areyan Viqueira,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复拍卖竞价中强化学习面临的"零坍塌"失败模式——由于拍卖奖励景观具有不连续、悬崖般的特性（如一价拍卖中低于阈值奖励为零），随机探索和梯度更新会导致策略越过最优高奖励区域陷入零奖励平坦区，且因缺乏梯度信号而难以恢复。作者通过机制分析、随机性与步长交互分析及多种算法实验验证了这一现象，并提出了初始化与架构方面的缓解策略，同时建立了拍卖环境的正式RL框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示重复拍卖竞价中强化学习方法因奖励景观不连续性而产生的根本性失败模式（零坍塌），并解释其机理，为改进算法提供理论依据。

Method: (1) 机制分析：解释不连续奖励如何导致梯度信号消失和零坍塌；(2) 策略随机性与步长交互分析；(3) 在REINFORCE和多种actor-critic变体上进行实证验证；(4) 提出初始化与架构选择的缓解策略；(5) 建立形式化的拍卖环境RL框架。

Result: 发现actor-critic方法尤其容易受到零坍塌影响，因为偏置的价值估计会加速向不稳定区域的移动；不连续奖励导致平坦零奖励区域与尖锐边界，使得陷入后的恢复极其样本低效。

Conclusion: 零坍塌是重复拍卖竞价中RL的核心失败模式，需通过合理的初始化与架构设计来缓解；拍卖环境的独特结构特性需要专门的RL框架来形式化描述。

Abstract: Bidding in repeated auctions is a central challenge for reinforcement learning (RL), combining continuous control with the strategic complexities of digital advertising. While policy gradient and value-based methods seem well-suited for these settings, they often struggle with the discontinuous, "cliff-like" nature of auction reward landscapes. In a first-price auction, for example, a bidder receives zero reward until they cross a specific threshold, after which the reward decreases as the bid increases. This creates a landscape of flat, zero-reward regions separated by sharp boundaries.
  We identify a fundamental failure mode in this setting termed "zero collapse." We show that stochastic exploration and gradient-based updates can cause policies to overshoot optimal high-reward regions and enter flat, zero-reward regimes. Once there, the lack of an informative gradient signal makes recovery extremely sample-inefficient, effectively trapping the agent. We find that actor-critic methods are particularly susceptible, as biased value estimates can accelerate this movement toward unstable regions.
  Our contributions include: (1) a mechanistic explanation of how discontinuous rewards lead to vanishing signals and zero collapse; (2) an analysis of the interaction between policy stochasticity and step size; and (3) an empirical demonstration of this phenomenon across REINFORCE and actor-critic variants. We propose practical mitigation strategies involving initialization and architectural choices to improve stability. Finally, we introduce a formal RL framework for auction environments highlighting their unique structural properties.

</details>


### [156] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Distilled Explanation Model (DEM)，一种三阶段玻璃盒框架，通过将梯度提升专家模型的知识蒸馏到基于残差的可解释决策树中，实现生理异常检测的高精度与可解释性。在四个数据集上AUC达0.9964，推理速度比SHAP快1235倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理传感器异常检测方法面临两难：黑盒模型性能强但不可解释，事后解释方法（如SHAP、LIME）计算开销大且解释真实性存疑。亟需一种兼具高预测精度与内在可解释性的方法，满足临床实时监控需求。

Method: DEM包含三个阶段：(1) 线性基线模型拟合；(2) XGBoost梯度提升专家模型学习非线性残差；(3) 将专家模型的非线性知识蒸馏到控制深度的可解释决策树中。创新包括：蒸馏保真度指标量化解释可信度，以及深度可控的精度-可解释性权衡机制。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、WESAD、eICU和SmartNet四个数据集上，临床异常检测AUC达0.9964，压力检测AUC达0.9047。推理时间0.17ms/千样本，比SHAP快1235倍。消融实验验证了蒸馏步骤的有效性，深度敏感性分析展示了可控的精度-可解释性权衡。

Conclusion: DEM是首个在生理异常检测中实现预测即解释的玻璃盒框架，其蒸馏保真度指标为解释可信度提供了量化保障，且实时推理能力使其适用于临床可穿戴监控场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [157] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不显式追踪认知不确定性的RLVR/GRPO更新为何有效，通过分析退火softmax策略在贝叶斯伯努利多臂老虎机中的表现，证明在β-正则性（β=1）下，该策略能达到近最优的贝叶斯遗憾率$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$，其有效性依赖于近最优臂的丰富性，为RLVR提供了结构类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何RLVR和GRPO等不包含显式不确定性追踪机制的策略更新方法仍然有效。

Method: 在贝叶斯伯努利多臂老虎机中，分析退火softmax（玻尔兹曼）策略的表现；针对先验满足线性上尾条件（β-正则性中β=1的情形，即近最优臂丰富）的情况，推导贝叶斯遗憾界。

Result: 退火softmax贪心策略在臂数$m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$时达到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$的贝叶斯遗憾，为近最优率；而在小臂数下可能遭受线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 此类不确定性不可知更新的有效性依赖于近最优臂的丰富性（β-正则性），为RLVR提供了结构类比：基线策略以非零概率生成正确回答扮演了β-正则性的角色。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [158] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHECKMATE通过代码进化自动生成求解算法，只需用户提供形式化规范（what）而无需专家设计求解过程（how），在配置和调度等工业问题上超越现有最先进求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模工业组合优化问题通常需要专家精心设计求解器和启发式策略，即不仅要指定"解是什么"，还要指定"如何求解"。作者希望消除这种对专家知识的依赖，实现仅凭形式化规范自动生成高效算法。

Method: 提出CHECKMATE工具，利用代码进化（code evolution）自动生成算法。核心思想是：形式化规范确保生成程序的正确性并支持系统性能评估，自然语言描述引导进化过程。算法生成仅依赖"what"（问题规范），无需人工指定"how"（求解方法）。

Result: 在配置和调度两个工业领域的选定问题上，CHECKMATE进化生成的算法在所有测试案例中均持续超越最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 形式化方法在引导代码进化以自动解决复杂现实问题方面具有巨大潜力，标志着从需要专家设计求解过程到仅需问题规范这一范式转变。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [159] [Automating Formal Verification with Reinforcement Learning and Recursive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30914)
*Max Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了如何通过强化学习（RLVR）和验证器引导的推理搜索来提升大语言模型生成经过形式化验证的程序和证明的能力。在Dafny上使用GRPO训练将验证通过率从2.2%提升至58.1%，但出现了规约攻击问题；过滤后多轮RLVR将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%。在Lean上开发了验证器引导的推理脚手架，将初始验证通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%，并解决了42个未解决问题中的7个。同时引入了仓库级Lean基准测试Dalek-Bench，初步结果较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自动化形式化验证方面面临挑战：形式化验证数据稀缺，且正确性依赖于精确的机器可检查规约而非生成合理的代码。现有方法难以生成符合严格规约的已验证程序和证明。

Method: 1) 在Dafny上使用GRPO及其变体进行RLVR训练，通过编译器/验证器结果评分组装完整程序；2) 多轮RLVR并过滤规约不完善的任务；3) 在Lean中构建验证器引导的推理脚手架，将证明生成分解为子目标搜索，结合验证器反馈、诊断和修复。

Result: Dafny实验：初始RLVR将验证奖励从2.2%提升至58.1%；过滤后多轮RLVR将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%。Lean实验：带证明修改器的完整脚手架将验证通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%；在VERINA数据集上解决了42个未解决问题中的7个。Dalek-Bench初步结果较弱。

Conclusion: 验证器环境结合RLVR和推理搜索能显著提升LLM生成已验证程序的能力，但存在规约攻击问题；仓库级基准测试仍需更强的进度评估和任务特定工具使用策略。

Abstract: Automated formal verification remains challenging for large language models because data for proof assistants and verification-aware languages is scarce, and correctness depends on satisfying precise machine-checkable specifications rather than producing plausible code. This thesis studies how verifier environments can improve LLM generation of verified programs and proofs through reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) and verifier-guided inference-time search. First, we train open-source models in Dafny with RLVR using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related variants, assembling generated candidates into complete programs and scoring them with compiler and verifier outcomes. Initial experiments on an APPS-derived Dafny dataset increased verified reward from 2.2% to 58.1%, but revealed specification hacking, where models exploit weak formal specifications instead of implementing the intended solutions. After filtering underspecified and vulnerable tasks, multi-turn RLVR on the refined benchmark improves the verified pass rate from 9.7% to 31.1%. Second, we develop a verifier-guided inference scaffold in Lean that treats proof generation as structured search over decomposed subgoals, verifier feedback, diagnostics, and repair. With a fixed base model, the full scaffold with proof reviser improves pass rate on an initial VeriCoding pilot set from 46.2% under direct repair to 69.2%. On the larger VERINA dataset, whole-task decomposition plus proof reviser solves 7 of 42 previously unsolved tasks. We also introduce Dalek-Bench, a repository-scale Lean benchmark derived from the Rust $\texttt{curve25519-dalek}$ verification project; preliminary results remain weak, indicating that stronger progress evaluation and task-specific tool-use policies are still needed.

</details>


### [160] [Unsupervised Diffusion Solver for Combinatorial Optimization via Combinatorial Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30920)
*Shengyu Feng,Tarun Suresh,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出组合伴随匹配（CAM），一种基于连续时间马尔可夫链和离散伴随动力学的无监督扩散求解器训练框架，在组合优化问题上性能优于现有无监督基线，接近有监督扩散求解器和传统求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器通常依赖有监督训练，需要大量近优解作为标注数据，这限制了其实用性。本文旨在设计一种无需最优解标注的无监督训练方法。

Method: 将扩散组合优化建模为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，引入离散伴随动力学来传播优化信号，提出组合伴随匹配（CAM）无监督训练框架，提供结构化且低方差的轨迹级优化信号。

Result: CAM在多个组合优化问题上持续优于现有无监督扩散基线，并与强监督扩散求解器及传统求解器性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过离散伴随方法有效地将无监督训练引入扩散组合优化求解器，弥合了无监督与有监督方法之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers have shown strong promise for combinatorial optimization (CO), but existing methods typically rely on supervised training with large collections of near-optimal solutions. In this work, we extend adjoint-based trajectory optimization methods to discrete combinatorial domains. We formulate diffusion-based CO as a stochastic control problem over Continuous-Time Markov Chains and introduce discrete adjoint dynamics for propagating optimization signals through discrete generative trajectories. Building on this formulation, we propose Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM), an unsupervised training framework for discrete diffusion solvers with structured and low-variance trajectory-level optimization signals. Empirically, CAM consistently outperforms existing unsupervised diffusion baselines and achieves performance competitive with strong supervised diffusion solvers and even traditional solvers across diverse combinatorial optimization problems. Our code is available at https://github.com/Shengyu-Feng/CAM.

</details>


### [161] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出信任域行为混合（TRB）方法，通过将早期rollout策略替换为教师最近的行为策略（在学生为中心的KL信任域内），并逐步退火KL预算至零，解决在线策略蒸馏中早期学生rollout质量差导致教师监督弱的问题，在数学推理蒸馏任务中取得最优平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过让学生在其自身策略采样的前缀上进行训练来解决离线蒸馏的前缀不匹配问题，但早期学生rollout质量仍然很差，导致教师监督建立在弱或低质量前缀上。

Method: 提出信任域行为混合（TRB）预热方法：在学生为中心的KL信任域内，将早期rollout策略替换为最接近教师的行为策略，同时保持每个前缀上的反向KL OPD损失不变；KL预算逐步退火至零，使训练在预热后回归纯学生rollout。

Result: 在两个数学推理蒸馏设置中，TRB在所有比较方法中取得了最强的平均性能。

Conclusion: TRB通过信任域约束的行为策略混合有效缓解了在线蒸馏早期阶段的学生rollout质量问题，是一种有效的预热方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [162] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现量子高斯过程（QGP）中两个看似无关的负面结果——HHL-based QGP回归的指数加速被排除和量子核的后验病态——实际上受同一量控制：核Gram矩阵的归一化谱熵S(K)/log n。作者证明了Nyström近似的柯西-施瓦茨尾界、基于Bach自由度的有限样本方差收缩恒等式，以及目标依赖的最优熵的表征。实验上该诊断是核无关的，多种量子与经典核族在去量子化、ECE和方差收缩面板上塌缩为相同曲线。该诊断成功从模拟器迁移到IBM Heron硬件，中位绝对误差3.2%，无需任何纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子高斯过程（QGP）领域近期出现了两个看似无关的重要结果：一是HHL-based QGP回归在典型良态条件下无法实现指数加速，二是高表达性量子核存在后验病态破坏贝叶斯优化。作者希望揭示这两个现象背后的统一机制，并提供一个可迁移的诊断工具来评估量子高斯过程在实际硬件上的表现。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明核Gram矩阵的归一化谱熵S(K)/log n是控制Nyström近似误差（Cauchy-Schwarz尾界）、方差收缩（Bach自由度d_σ(K)恒等式）和目标依赖最优熵（目标在核本征基下的本征维度）的统一量。2) 实验验证：在多种核族（硬件高效、matchgate、IQP、RBF/Matérn/RFF/深度核）上验证S/log n曲线的普适性。3) 硬件迁移：将诊断从模拟器迁移到IBM Heron量子处理器，测试24种配置（n_q=4）及n_q=6的扩展，未使用任何纠错。

Result: 1) 所有核族在去量子化、ECE和方差收缩面板上塌缩为相同的S/log n曲线。2) NLL最佳点位于高熵（光滑目标）或低熵（带限量子数据目标）。3) 从模拟器到IBM Heron硬件的迁移：n_q=4的24种配置中位绝对误差3.2%，均值5.2%；Matchgate和IQP核均在5%以内；仅一个HE配置出现30%异常值（重跑降至0.5%，归因于校准漂移）。4) 迁移到第二个Heron后端误差2.7%，扩展到n_q=6时在原后端误差1.7%。5) 全程未使用任何纠错。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵S(K)/log n是量子高斯过程中一个普适且有效的诊断量，统一解释了HHL加速被排除和量子核后验病态这两个看似无关的现象。该诊断不仅核无关，还能从模拟器可靠迁移到真实量子硬件（IBM Heron），在无需纠错的情况下保持低误差，为量子高斯过程的实际应用提供了有价值的理论指导和实践工具。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [163] [Revisiting Zeroth-Order Hessian Approximation: A Single-Step Policy Optimization Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30960)
*Junbin Qiu,Zhaowei Hong,Renzhe Xu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZoVH框架，通过策略优化的视角统一了零阶Hessian估计方法，推导了最优基线以最小化方差，并利用查询重用策略提升样本效率，在理论上证明了无偏性和收敛保证，实验验证了其在各种应用中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维设置下获取完整的低方差Hessian及其逆的估计器仍然是一个重大挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的框架来系统性地降低方差。

Method: 通过单步策略优化的视角重新解释ZO Hessian近似，建立理论等价性；提出ZoVH套件，包含两种关键技术：(1) 推导唯一最优基线以理论最小化方差，(2) 查询重用策略利用历史函数查询提升样本效率。

Result: 理论分析确认了Hessian估计器的无偏性、基线的方差最优性，提供了整个ZoVH套件的误差界以及曲率感知ZO算法的收敛保证；大量实验验证了估计精度和收敛性能的优越性。

Conclusion: ZoVH通过统一框架和方差缩减技术，显著提升了零阶Hessian估计的准确性和效率，为无需梯度信息的优化任务提供了实用且理论完备的工具。

Abstract: Accurate Zeroth-Order (ZO) Hessian estimation is a cornerstone of derivative-free methods, essential for tasks such as bilevel optimization, Bayesian inference, and uncertainty quantification. However, obtaining a complete suite of low-variance estimators for the Hessian and its inverse in high-dimensional settings remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a unified framework that reinterprets ZO Hessian approximation through the lens of single-step Policy Optimization (PO). This perspective establishes a theoretical equivalence between general ZO Hessian estimators and the Hessian of a smoothed PO objective, unifying distinct classical randomized estimators as specific instances of baseline selection. Building on this foundation, we introduce ZoVH, a comprehensive suite of variance-reduced estimators for the full Hessian matrix, its regularized inverse, and the bias-corrected inverse Hessian-gradient product. ZoVH leverages two key techniques: (1) a unique optimal baseline derived to provably minimize variance, and (2) a query reuse strategy that incorporates historical function queries to enhance sample efficiency without inflating costs. Our rigorous theoretical analysis confirms the unbiasedness of the Hessian estimator, validates the variance optimality of our baseline, provides error bounds for the entire ZoVH suite, and establishes convergence guarantees for the resulting curvature-aware ZO algorithm. Extensive empirical results validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that ZoVH achieves superior estimation accuracy and convergence performance in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/Qjbtiger/ZoVH

</details>


### [164] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦TinyML中的分布变化问题，通过分析约70篇在线学习（ODL）文献，揭示了不同分布变化类型对设备端应用、硬件和解决方案结构的影响，并指出了方法基准与真实部署场景之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献未系统描述分布变化如何发生，以及不同变化类型需要不同解决方案，该综述旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以"分布变化机制"为统一原则，调研约70篇在线学习（ODL）相关工作，分析不同分布变化类型对应用、硬件和解决方案结构的影响。

Result: 揭示了不同分布变化类型对设备端应用、硬件选择和解决方案结构的具体影响，并识别出方法基准与真实部署之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: 分布变化是TinyML在线学习的核心挑战，不同类型分布变化需要差异化解决方案，当前基准测试与真实场景间存在显著鸿沟。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [165] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LVCG，首个在物理隐含的向量心电图（VCG）空间中进行自监督表示学习的框架，通过学习视图不变的低维VCG表征替代原始12导联ECG空间中的冗余表示，从而提升泛化能力和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG表示学习方法均在可观测的ECG信号空间中进行，但标准12导联ECG本质上是同一心电活动在不同空间方位的投影，存在大量冗余信息，易导致虚假相关和过拟合风险。

Method: 受Frank向量心电图（VCG）模型启发，提出LVCG框架，在物理可解释的VCG潜在空间中学习统一的、视角不变的潜在心电活动表征，取代导联特定的ECG空间表征。

Result: LVCG在多个任务上全面优于基于ECG空间的基线方法，尤其在领域偏移场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在VCG空间中进行自监督表示学习可以有效减少冗余、避免虚假相关，是提升心电表征学习质量的有效范式。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [166] [The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31289)
*Amir Esterhuysen,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为终端表征（Terminal Representation, TR）的新型表征学习方法，它编码奖励加权轨迹，无需特征分解即可直接用于下游任务，且计算开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继任表征（SR）和默认表征（DR）是强化学习中两种重要的空间-时间抽象工具，但DR需要通过特征分解来支持下游任务，这带来了对称转移动力学假设和高计算开销的问题。本文旨在提出一种结构上不同的表征形式，以克服这些局限性。

Method: 提出终端表征（TR），它像DR一样编码奖励加权轨迹，但具有更低的维度，可直接用于下游任务而无需特征分解。文章建立了TR的理论基础，包括推导、两种学习算法的收敛性证明、零样本组合性，以及替代奖励公式之间的等价关系。还证明TR嵌入在DR的顶部特征向量中。

Result: 理论上证明了TR的收敛性、组合性及其与DR顶部特征向量的嵌入关系；实验上验证了TR在辅助应用中作为现有表征可行替代的有效性，且学习、存储和使用的计算开销更低。

Conclusion: TR是一种新颖的表征形式，能够在不依赖特征分解的前提下捕获与DR相同的底层知识，在多个下游任务中表现良好且计算效率更高，是SR和DR的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Representation learning is a powerful tool for spatio-temporal abstraction within reinforcement learning (RL). Two well established approaches are through the successor representation (SR) and the default representation (DR). The SR encodes states by the future trajectories they induce, capturing information flow decoupled from reward. The DR builds on this by weighting trajectories with reward, integrating credit-assignment structure into the representation. Eigenvectors of both representations have been used to support a range of downstream tasks -- including option discovery, reward shaping, transfer learning, and exploration. We introduce a structurally distinct formulation: the terminal representation (TR). The TR encodes reward-weighted trajectories similarly to the DR, but can be learned as a lower-dimensionality object, and can be used directly for the mentioned applications without eigenvector computations. Eigendecomposition also imposes the assumption of symmetric transition dynamics, which the TR can bypass. In this work we develop the theoretical foundations of the TR: its derivation, convergence of two learning algorithms, its use for zero-shot compositionality, and equivalences between alternative reward formulations. We further show the TR is embedded in the top DR eigenvector, allowing it to capture the same underlying knowledge without eigendecomposition. Additionally, we provide empirical evidence of the TR as a viable alternative to existing representations in subsidiary applications, while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and use.

</details>


### [167] [An Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31016)
*Yulin Hu,Fuyan Ou,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效可扩展的图压缩方法SP-ESGC，通过解耦设计将节点压缩与图结构生成分离，采用热核特征传播、混合聚类策略和预训练边预测器，在保持结构的同时实现了高计算效率和跨GNN架构的良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法存在两方面问题：一是耦合优化导致计算效率低下，二是跨GNN架构泛化能力差。因此需要一种高效、可扩展且结构保持的图压缩方法。

Method: 采用解耦设计：(1) 利用热核特征传播（基于谱图理论扩散）生成节点表示；(2) 设计新型混合聚类策略从节点表示中提取判别性类内质心；(3) 使用预训练边预测器从原始图中推断可迁移的结构模式，生成准确的合成图。

Result: 在真实世界图数据集上的实验表明，SP-ESGC实现了精确的图压缩，同时显著提高了计算效率，并且能够很好地泛化到不同的GNN架构。

Conclusion: SP-ESGC通过解耦设计和结构保持策略，有效解决了现有图压缩方法的计算效率低和跨架构泛化差的问题，为资源受限场景下GNN部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graph condensation (GC) is pivotal for enabling Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) deployment in resource-constrained scenarios by compressing large-scale graphs into compact synthetic counterparts. Existing GC methods commonly suffer from computational inefficiency due to coupled optimization as well as encountering poor generalization across GNN architectures. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving (SP-ESGC), which possesses a decoupled design that separates node condensation from graph structure generation. Specifically, it first employs heat kernel feature propagation to generate node representation via spectral graph theory-inspired diffusion. Further, a novel hybrid clustering strategy is designed to extracts discriminative intra-class centroids from the node representation. Finally, a pre-trained edge predictor infers transferable structural patterns from the original graph, ensuring accurate synthetic graph generation. Extensive experiments on real-world graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed SP-ESGC implementes a precise GC with significantly high computational efficiency. Moreover, SP-ESGC also generalizes well across diverse GNN architectures.

</details>


### [168] [dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360)
*David Fernández-Narro,Pablo Ferri,Ángel Sánchez-García,Juan M. García-Gómez,Carlos Sáez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为dashi的开源Python库，用于探索、量化和表征数据集偏移（dataset shifts），包括无监督（基于信息几何和非参数统计流形）和有监督（量化模型性能退化）两种方法，并在三个医疗AI案例（妊娠期糖尿病、COVID-19、紧急医疗调度）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集偏移（包括时间偏移和多源偏移）会严重损害AI模型性能和数据质量，尤其在医疗健康领域涉及患者安全和基本权利。尽管协变量偏移、先验偏移和概念偏移的理论基础已较为成熟，但缺乏易于使用且功能全面的软件工具来执行相关分析。

Method: dashi库提供双重视角：1) 无监督方法：利用信息几何和非参数统计流形进行数据变异性表征，包括信息几何时序图、全局概率偏差和源概率离群度等指标；2) 有监督方法：量化并表征模型性能退化。两种方法均支持用户自定义的时间批次和域/源批次。

Result: 在三个模拟和真实世界的医疗AI案例研究（妊娠期糖尿病、COVID-19、紧急医疗调度）中验证了dashi的实用性，展示了其交互式可视化分析能力和变异性度量指标的有效性。

Conclusion: dashi通过评估数据一致性和AI性能，支持AI生命周期各阶段的可信度，助力构建鲁棒且安全的机器学习管道。

Abstract: The Artificial Intelligence (AI) life cycle requires a thorough understanding of the underlying data dynamics for robust, safe and cost-effective AI development and use. Dataset shifts are defined as changes between train and test data distributions. Whether occurring over time (temporal) or across different sites (multi-source), they can severely degrade model performance and compromise data quality. This is particularly important in health AI, where the safety and fundamental rights of patients can be severely affected by uncontrolled shifts both at training and operational stages. While the theoretical foundations of covariate, prior, and concept shifts are well established, there is a lack of accessible and comprehensive software tools to perform their analysis. We introduce dashi, an open-source Python library designed for the exploration, quantification, and characterization of dataset shifts. dashi provides a dual approach: an unsupervised approach that leverages information geometry and non-parametric statistical manifolds to data variability characterization and analysis (e.g., Information Geometric Temporal plots and Multi-Source Variability metrics like Global Probabilistic Deviation and Source Probabilistic Outlyingness), and a supervised approach that quantifies and characterizes model performance degradation. Both unsupervised and supervised approaches work across user-defined temporal and domain/source batches. We demonstrate the utility of dashi on three simulated and real-world health AI case studies on gestational diabetes mellitus, COVID-19 and emergency medical dispatch. By providing interactive visual analytics and variability metrics, dashi supports trustworthiness of AI life cycle stages enabling robust and safe machine learning pipelines through the assessment of data coherence and AI performance.

</details>


### [169] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL提出了一种多模态预训练框架，通过联合利用RTL代码与其控制数据流图（CDFG）表示，实现细粒度的跨模态对齐，在性能预测和代码检索任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL设计表示方法通常仅依赖单一数据模态（RTL代码或基于图的表示），限制了学习表示的表达能力和泛化能力。作者认为整合代码和CDFG这两种互补模态对于全面理解RTL设计至关重要。

Method: UniRTL通过联合掩码建模实现代码与CDFG的细粒度对齐，并采用分层训练策略，包括预训练的图感知分词器、文本（功能摘要）与代码的阶段式对齐，最后进行图集成。

Result: 在性能预测和代码检索两个下游任务的多种设置下，UniRTL一致地优于先前方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性和表达能力。

Conclusion: UniRTL为硬件设计自动化建立了一个更强大、更鲁棒的基础，验证了多模态RTL表示学习的有效性。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>


### [170] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent任务效率（ATE）概念，并构建了面向AI编码Agent的原生MoE训练框架PithTrain，在保持与生产框架相当吞吐量的同时，显著降低了Agent完成训练框架开发和维护任务的成本（Agent Turn减少62%，GPU活跃时间减少64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE训练框架经过多年工程优化，但难以适应新架构和系统优化。AI编码Agent虽有潜力自动化部分开发工作，但应用于现有框架时存在未被当前吞吐量评估体系捕捉的隐性成本，亟需一种能衡量Agent与框架交互效率的新指标。

Method: 提出Agent任务效率（ATE）这一新维度指标，基于四项Agent原生设计原则构建了轻量级MoE训练框架PithTrain，并设计了ATE-Bench基准测试集，涵盖真实训练框架开发任务。

Result: PithTrain在生产吞吐量上与现有框架持平；在ATE-Bench上，相比现有框架，Agent Turn减少62%，Active GPU Time减少64%，显著提升了Agent任务效率。

Conclusion: 面向AI Agent原生设计的训练框架能够大幅降低Agent理解和扩展框架的成本，为未来训练框架的发展提供了以Agent效率为导向的新设计范式。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [171] [On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500)
*Daria Fomina,Daniil Krasylnikov,Alexey Boykov,Andrey Dolgovyazov,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Fedor Velikonivtsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从I/O和算术强度角度，将GNN层分为三类（SpMM卷积、归约聚合、注意力层），为每类开发了优化GPU内核，实现了显著加速（最高8.5-10倍）和内存减少（最高76倍），并作为即插即用替代方案开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN框架（如DGL、PyTorch Geometric）因稀疏不规则的内存访问导致性能瓶颈，复杂层常物化边级中间变量增加内存流量，限制了在大图上的可扩展性。现有工作缺乏对GNN层从I/O和算术强度角度的系统性分析和优化。

Method: 1) 将GNN层归类为三种内核族：基于SpMM的卷积、基于归约的聚合、基于注意力的层（GATv2/Graph Transformer）。2) 针对每类设计减少数据移动、改善局部性的GPU内核（包括Tensor Core块稀疏变体）。3) 研究图重排序对不同内核映射的影响。

Result: 融合注意力内核：Graph Transformer达3.9倍加速（中位1.6倍），Tensor Core变体在局部密集图上达7.3倍；GATv2达8.5倍加速（中位2.0倍），峰值内存减少76倍（中位6倍）。度感知归约内核达10倍加速（中位2.6倍）。SpMM层缓存优化的cuSPARSE比DGL快8倍。

Conclusion: 通过I/O和算术强度导向的核心理念，不同GNN层族可系统性地获得大幅加速和内存优化；图重排序效果取决于内核映射方式，对邻居并行（gather主导）内核更有利；所有优化以即插即用形式发布，支持可复现的硬件感知GNN加速。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [172] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReuseRL方法，基于MDL原则通过技能字典和分割代价惩罚提升LLM智能体的泛化能力，在多个环境中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练的LLM智能体常学到脆弱的、任务特定的捷径，泛化能力差。作者假设当成功轨迹在结构上可压缩（分解为少量可复用的抽象模式）时，智能体能更好地泛化。

Method: 基于最小描述长度(MDL)原则，从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典，并在RL目标中加入分割代价，显式惩罚编码效率低的 idiosyncratic 行为。同时给出了该压缩惩罚的PAC-Bayes泛化界证明。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个环境中，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外场景下的成功率均优于vanilla GRPO及强round-length基线方法。

Conclusion: 压缩正则化能有效提升LLM智能体的泛化能力，MDL原则为强化学习中的泛化提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [173] [Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558)
*Felipe Urrutia,Juan José Alegría,Cinthia Sanchez Macias,Jorge Salas,Cristian B. Calderon,Cristobal Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过训练GPT-J模型在两种结构等价的多跳推理任务上，研究了注意力头在学习过程中分化为位置型（positional）和符号型（symbolic）两种纯化头（pure heads）的动力学机制，并揭示了它们在长序列外推能力上的定量差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer在结构化任务中的求解机制及其在未见场景下的行为预测，对于模型的安全部署至关重要。

Method: 在GPT-J模型上训练数字位置推理和字母符号推理两种结构等价的多跳推理任务；利用分类指标将注意力头分为位置型和符号型；通过理论构造展示单层RoPE注意力如何通过几何可解释的QKV操作实现这些功能；引入"差异量"（discrepancy）概念形式化两种机制在长序列鲁棒性上的差异。

Result: 成功学习与纯化头的出现相关；数字任务需要位置型和符号型两种头部，字母任务仅需符号型头部；符号机制在长序列外推上更可靠，位置机制面临更尖锐的局限性。

Conclusion: 注意力头会分化为位置或符号两种纯化头；两种机制在长序列泛化能力上存在定量分离，符号机制更具外推优势。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.

</details>


### [174] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自动选择少量多样检索器组合（portfolio）的RAG方法，通过期望最优目标函数和高效构建算法，在多个QA基准上优于单检索器和简单多检索器基线，并通过并行检索降低延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常使用单一检索器和固定超参数，但实际查询高度异构（从简单事实到复杂多跳推理），单一配置难以覆盖多样化的查询分布。

Method: 形式化定义为查询分布上的期望最优目标；从大量候选检索器中自动选择少量多样子集（portfolio）；设计高效构建算法并保证近似最优；学习路由管道将查询分配给最合适的检索器。

Result: 在多个QA基准上，学习得到的组合和路由管道在检索指标和答案质量上均优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线；相比推理时超参数调优，固定组合通过并行检索和LLM调用实现了相当或更好的准确率，同时显著降低延迟和token成本。

Conclusion: 检索器组合策略能有效覆盖异构查询分布，兼顾性能与效率，为RAG系统的实际部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [175] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlagGAM提出了一种基于规则定义基函数的框架，将特征级别的规则构建与预测分离，通过稀疏可读的单变量基函数（阈值标志、类别标志、尾部偏离基、分类阶梯函数）和可选的灵活预测头，在表格数据上实现了接近EBM的透明加性模型表现，同时显著优于岭回归，并在缺失和噪声扰动下表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的表格预测任务需要模型同时具备准确性、透明性和对不完美输入的鲁棒性，现有方法难以兼顾三者。

Method: 提出FlagGAM框架，包含：(1) Flag Core Module，将数值和类别变量转换为稀疏、人类可读的单变量基函数（阈值标志、类别级标志、尾部偏离基、分类阶梯函数）；(2) 默认加性头作为受限GAM风格预测器，可替换为灵活预测头；(3) 保留稀疏规则基矩阵而非压缩为计数汇总，支持混合类型分类/回归、特征特异性加权。

Result: 在表格基准上，默认FlagGAM在透明加性模式下接近EBM；在混合类型回归上显著优于岭回归；在缺失和噪声扰动下AUROC下降幅度小于常见基线。使用灵活预测头可进一步提升精度，接近强树模型基线。

Conclusion: FlagGAM为需要竞争性精度、可沟通规则和对不完美输入鲁棒性的表格任务提供了实用的中间方案，但灵活头模式下应解释为规则基表示后接非线性预测器而非完全加性GAM。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [176] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于几何的多模态融合方法(GMF)，利用扩散薛定谔桥传输的平方初始速度作为数据质量评判标准，打破传统置信度方法的循环依赖问题，显著提升对传感器噪声和语义冲突的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信融合方法依赖模型自身预测置信度来评判数据质量，存在循环依赖：当模型自信但预测错误时，这些方法无法检测到误差。

Method: 提出GMF方法，不依赖预测结果，而是通过测量输入在潜空间中所需的传输校正量来评估可靠性。具体实现为采用带整流流的扩散薛定谔桥传输，将平方初始速度作为高效学习的校正分数：有效数据具有低平方速度幅值，而噪声、不完整或冲突数据需要更强的传输校正。

Result: 实验证明GMF在严重传感器噪声和语义冲突场景下显著优于基于置信度的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于几何的可靠性信号可作为独立评判标准，即使在分类器被欺骗的情况下也能有效标记不可靠输入，打破循环依赖。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [177] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFRe框架，通过固定点求解器替代部分去噪器，结合交叉步一致性损失和三状态重用策略，在减少参数、训练时间和显存的同时，提升掩码生成模型在低采样预算下的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码生成模型（MGMs）在每一步都需要全序列双向Transformer，导致训练成本高且低采样预算下质量下降。现有方法通过更好的采样器或固定深度去噪器提升效率，但每步仍分配固定计算量。本文旨在通过自适应深度和更少的参数来改善质量-成本权衡。

Method: 提出固定点掩码生成模型（FP-MGMs），用共享注意力层上的固定点求解器替代部分去噪器实现自适应深度；引入交叉步一致性损失（对齐相邻去噪步的隐藏表示）和三状态重用（3SR，对未变化、仍掩码、新揭示的token分别处理来热启动求解器）；形成完整训练到推理框架CoFRe，并支持预训练MGMs微调转换。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，参数减少38.8%，训练时间减少11.5%，显存减少16.9%，在96个Transformer块前向传播预算下生成困惑度从830.8降至101.8；在ImageNette上，训练时间减少48.6%，显存减少50.7%，所有采样预算下FID均有改善。

Conclusion: CoFRe提供了更廉价训练和更强低预算掩码生成的实用框架，验证了固定点方法在生成模型中的有效性。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [178] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL)，通过投影将一维散度扩展到多维回报分布，解决多变量分布强化学习中的收缩和计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布强化学习在扩展到多变量场景时面临挑战：常见度量难以自然推广到一维以上或失去计算可行性，且多变量情况下的广义矩阵折扣缺乏收缩保证。

Method: 通过切片（slicing）技术将可计算的一维散度投影到多维回报分布上；提出均匀切片下的共享标量折扣Bellman收缩，以及最大切片变体在稠密折扣矩阵下的收缩保证；分析Wasserstein、Cramér和MMD三种基散度，刻画适合标准单样本Bellman更新的SDRL变体。

Result: 在玩具链问题、基于图像的网格世界环境以及部分Atari游戏上进行了评估。

Conclusion: SDRL通过切片技术有效解决了多变量分布强化学习的维度扩展和收缩问题，支持多种基散度并保持了计算可行性。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [179] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种将KL正则化强化学习目标转化为重要性加权监督学习的框架，通过从固定参考策略采样离线轨迹并计算回报重要性权重，以SFT的效率实现多轮交互优化，性能媲美在线强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互场景中，在线强化学习虽能有效处理动态交互但训练成本高昂（每次更新需生成完整校正轨迹），而离线SFT虽高效但存在分布偏移和行为崩塌问题。亟需一种兼具两者优势的方法。

Method: DRIFT基于KL正则化RL目标等价于重要性加权监督学习的理论洞见，将轨迹生成与优化解耦：(1) 从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹；(2) 计算基于回报的重要性权重；(3) 通过加权SFT优化策略。

Result: 实验表明DRIFT在多轮交互任务上匹配或超越多轮RL基线，同时保持标准SFT的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT成功弥合了在线RL与离线SFT之间的效率-效果鸿沟，为多轮交互场景提供了一种实用且高效的优化框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [180] [Toward Identifiable Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31245)
*Walter Nelson,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出iSAE（可识别稀疏自编码器），通过架构和训练过程的微小改动，解决了标准SAE在不同训练运行间高度不稳定的问题，在降低重构误差的同时提升了稳定性，并从理论上将其与传统字典学习方法联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE在不同训练运行中产生不一致的概念词典和稀疏编码，导致可解释性和实用可靠性不足，需要提升其稳定性。

Method: 1. 理论分析了导致SAE不稳定的模型属性；2. 针对每个问题对架构和训练过程进行最小改动；3. 提出两种版本的iSAE（可识别SAE）；4. 从理论上将SAE与传统字典学习联系起来，并证明实际学习的字典满足近似受限等距条件(RIP)，使稀疏编码近似可识别。

Result: iSAE相比标准TopK SAE具有更低的重构误差和显著提升的稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析与最小化工程改动，iSAE有效解决了SAE的不稳定性问题，并在字典学习框架下获得了理论保证。

Abstract: Recently, sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as an attractive tool for interpreting and interacting with representations in practical neural networks. While it is common empirical folklore, we also show theoretically that SAEs are highly unstable: different training runs are likely to produce different concept dictionaries and sparse codes. We characterize the model properties that hinder the stability of real-world SAEs, and address each of these problems through minimal changes to the architecture and training procedure. Together, these changes yield two versions of an \textbf{i}dentifiable SAE (iSAE), a variant of the standard TopK SAE with lower reconstruction error and improved stability. We explain this improvement theoretically by connecting SAEs with traditional dictionary learning approaches, and show that the dictionaries learned in practice satisfy an approximate restricted isometry condition, rendering the corresponding sparse codes in those models near-identifiable.

</details>


### [181] [Fraud Type Decomposition and the Observation-Mechanism Taxonomy:Class-Specific Detection Limits in Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31257)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有欺诈检测将欺诈视为同质二元变量是结构性错误的，提出了将欺诈分为五类的观测机制分类法，证明按类别分别估计欺诈率并聚合严格优于统一估计，效率差距由Jensen惩罚刻画，并推导出每类检测的理论约束边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺诈检测方法将欺诈视为同质二元变量，忽略了欺诈标签来源于异构且不完美的观测过程，这一假设结构性错误导致可证明的低效。

Method: 提出观测机制分类法将欺诈分为五类，每类由不同的审查和标签流水线定义；通过理论分析证明按类别分别估计并聚合优于统一估计，用Jensen惩罚刻画效率差距；推导每类检测的理论约束（内生标签腐败、结构性不可观测性、特征非信息性）。

Result: 证明欺诈检测本质上是多个不同的估计问题集合，每类有其自身的观测结构和检测极限，按类别分别估计严格优于统一估计。

Conclusion: 欺诈检测不应被视为单一同质问题，而应视为受不同观测机制约束的多个估计问题的集合，需要针对每类设计相应的检测策略。

Abstract: Fraud detection in payment networks relies on labels generated through heterogeneous and imperfect observation processes, yet existing approaches treat fraud as a homogeneous binary variable. We show that this assumption is structurally incorrect and leads to provable inefficiency. We introduce an observation-mechanism taxonomy that partitions fraud into five classes, each defined by a distinct censorship and labeling pipeline. We prove that estimating fraud rates separately by class and aggregating strictly dominates pooled estimation, with the efficiency gap characterized as a Jensen penalty arising from heterogeneous observation rates. For each class, we derive the binding theoretical constraint on detection, including endogenous label corruption, structural non-observability, and feature non-informativeness. These results establish that fraud detection is fundamentally a collection of distinct estimation problems, each governed by its own observation structure and detection limit.

</details>


### [182] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高功率脉冲变换器故障检测，通过改变时序滤波和跨通道混合的顺序，并提出自适应通道重加权方法，在SNS的HVCM数据集上取得了SOTA性能（AUC-PR 0.816, AUC-ROC 0.934）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率脉冲变换器的非计划停机是大型加速器设施停机的主要原因，SNS的HVCM持续是第二大束流时间损失来源。现有深度学习方法从第一层就纠缠时序和跨通道操作，缺乏显式建模通道独立性和结构化交互的机制，因此需要探索架构归纳偏置对检测性能的影响。

Method: 改变时序滤波和跨通道混合的应用顺序，引入逐脉冲自适应通道重加权机制，在公开HVCM数据集上对四个子系统（RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL）进行实验评估。

Result: 最佳变体在全部子系统和六种故障家族中的五种上超越SOTA，池化AUC-PR为0.816，AUC-ROC为0.934。消融实验识别出三个主导输入通道，并将故障家族性能差异归因于故障前兆表现为单通道幅度偏移还是需要联合通道表征的细微模式。

Conclusion: 架构中时序滤波和跨通道混合的顺序对检测性能有重要影响，自适应通道重加权能进一步提升敏感度；不同故障类型的前兆表征模式不同，需要差异化的架构设计。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [183] [Survival Reinforcement Learning: Toward Scalable Self-Supervised RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31273)
*Franki Nguimatsia-Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生存强化学习（SRL），一种基于在线分类的替代方法，通过最大化智能体在目标状态的停留时间来解决对比强化学习（CRL）在长周期目标条件规划中面临的均匀性-容忍度困境，在机器人大范围运动任务上性能提升2-8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督对比强化学习（CRL）虽具有深度扩展能力，但在长周期目标条件规划中存在由对比损失固有的均匀性-容忍度困境导致的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出生存强化学习（SRL），一种基于在线分类的框架，扩展生存价值学习范式，通过最大化智能体在目标状态的停留时间（dwell time）来优化策略。SRL避开了CRL的结构约束，并缓解了生存框架中固有的"bang-bang"控制问题。

Result: 在多种机器人基准测试中，SRL在操作任务上与最先进的CRL相当，在稳定的长周期运动任务上性能提升2倍到8倍。

Conclusion: 分类方法可能成为扩展强化学习的关键原语，为突破CRL的局限性提供了新的方向。

Abstract: While self-supervised Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has shown remarkable depth-scaling capabilities, successfully using networks over 64 layers, scaled CRL still struggles with long-horizon goal-conditioned planning due to the uniformity-tolerance dilemma inherent in contrastive losses. We introduce Survival Reinforcement Learning (SRL), an online classification-based alternative that extends the survival value learning framework by maximizing the agent's dwell time at target goals. SRL bypasses the structural constraints of CRL and mitigates the "bang-bang" control solutions inherent to survival frameworks, which often induce undesirable behavior in complex dynamical systems. Evaluated across diverse robotic benchmarks, scaled SRL matches state-of-the-art CRL on manipulation tasks and outperforms it by 2x to 8x on stable, long-horizon locomotion tasks. Our results provide strong additional evidence that classification-based methods may serve as a key primitive in the broader effort to scale reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [184] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了机器遗忘中的一个新问题——局部性附带遗忘（localized collateral forgetting）：即使在聚合指标上表现良好的遗忘模型，在接近遗忘集的数据点上其预测与重训练模型存在显著且非均匀的偏差。作者通过分析发现，这一现象源于遗忘过程中使用的替代目标与重训练诱导的局部预测结构不一致，并通过共享表征传播到邻近样本。为此提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation），用仅在遗忘集的保留邻域上训练的小教师模型生成软标签替代随机目标，显著缩小了与重训练模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估方法通常使用聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数），这些指标可能掩盖局部区域的失败模式。作者希望研究遗忘模型在单个样本层面与重训练模型的预测差异，揭示被聚合指标隐藏的不均匀失效问题。

Method: 1) 在示例级别比较遗忘模型与重训练模型的逐点预测差异，发现该差异高度非均匀——靠近遗忘集的样本差异更大。2) 分析机制：遗忘使用的替代目标（如随机标签、梯度上升目标）与重训练模型在局部区域的预测结构不一致，通过共享表征传播。3) 提出Local Teacher Distillation：在遗忘集的保留邻域上训练一个小型教师模型，生成软标签作为遗忘目标，替代随机/硬目标。

Result: 在CIFAR-100局部类别删除任务上，相比基线方法，局部教师蒸馏显著缩小了遗忘模型与重训练模型在遗忘集附近样本上的预测差异，同时保持了有竞争力的聚合遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 聚合指标不足以揭示遗忘模型的局部失败模式；局部性附带遗忘是由替代目标与局部预测结构不一致导致的；局部教师蒸馏是一种简单有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>


### [185] [A Unifying View of Variational Generative Wasserstein Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31369)
*Paul Caucheteux,Clément Bonet,Anna Korba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成Wasserstein流（GWF）的统一理论框架，证明多种现有生成模型可视为参数化JKO方案的实例，并将该框架扩展至IPM和平方MMD，推导出新的JKO生成算法，最后分析了参数化Wasserstein流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型虽都可视为最小化分布之间的散度，但依赖不同的算法和几何原理，缺乏统一的理论视角。作者旨在建立基于Wasserstein梯度流的统一框架，揭示现有方法的内在联系。

Method: 1) 将生成模型建模为Wasserstein梯度流的参数化离散化（JKO方案）；2) 证明多种方法（如GAN变体）是f-散度目标下参数化JKO的实例；3) 将框架从f-散度扩展到IPM和平方MMD，推导新算法；4) 通过实证研究JKO正则化的影响；5) 分析参数化Wasserstein流。

Result: 建立了生成模型的统一理论框架，建立了多种近期提出算法之间的等价关系，推导了基于JKO的新生成算法，阐明了与GAN的联系，并通过实验验证了JKO正则化的效果。

Conclusion: GWF框架为生成模型提供了统一视角，不仅统一了现有方法，还拓展了新的生成算法设计空间，参数化Wasserstein流的分析为理解分布优化动力学提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>


### [186] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftSignum和SoftMuon，通过软符号变换替代硬符号操作，结合自适应温度调度和几何松弛框架，解决基于符号的优化器在终端收敛阶段的振荡问题，在多种深度学习任务（包括LLM预训练）中取得优于AdamW和硬符号优化器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号和LMO启发的优化器虽然内存占用低、性能强，但其固定幅度更新与梯度幅度解耦，无法处理参数异质性，导致终端收敛阶段出现振荡而非收敛。
