Method: 1）提出SoftSignum，用温度控制的软符号变换（soft-sign）替代硬符号映射，实现从符号更新到SGD类幅度敏感更新的逐参数平滑过渡；2）设计基于自适应分位数的温度调度策略；3）将相同原理扩展到矩阵值优化器得到SoftMuon；4）基于强凸正则化器和Fenchel共轭构建通用的几何松弛框架，并证明在随机非凸设置下的收敛性。

Result: 在多种深度学习任务（包括LLM预训练）上，SoftSignum和SoftMuon始终优于其对应的硬符号版本以及标准AdamW。

Conclusion: 软符号变换结合自适应温度调度能有效缓解符号优化器的终端收敛问题，所提出的几何松弛框架具有理论保证，在实际任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [187] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合最大最小准则与显式约束满足的多目标强化学习框架，在理论上建立基础，通过收敛性分析和表格实验验证算法，并在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理等实际场景中有效平衡了公平性与约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大最小MORL方法在需要融入显式约束的场景中适用性有限，因此需要一种能同时兼顾公平性（最大最小准则）与约束满足的MORL框架。

Method: 提出一种将最大最小准则与显式约束满足整合的MORL框架，进行理论基础构建，并通过收敛性分析及表格环境实验验证算法有效性。

Result: 在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理三个领域的实验中，所提方法有效平衡了公平性与约束满足。

Conclusion: 该框架为在约束条件下实现公平的多目标决策提供了一种可行的理论支撑与实用方法。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [188] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"通用Transformer"概念，即固定参数的Transformer可以通过适当的输入嵌入模拟给定类别中的任何其他Transformer，类似于通用图灵机。研究提供了稀疏构造和随机初始化的通用性证明，并通过实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer的表达能力来源，即其能力是否更多来自于输入表示而非学习到的权重参数。

Method: 提出通用Transformer概念，给出稀疏构造证明（在嵌入维度足够大时实现通用性），并证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性。

Result: 理论上证明了通用Transformer的存在性和通用性；实验上在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上验证了理论。

Conclusion: Transformer的表达能力很大程度上可能存在于输入表示中，而非其学习到的权重参数。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [189] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性向量自回归（NVAR）/下一代储层计算（NG-RC）模型在时间序列预测中的可辨识性问题，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率、非线性度及延迟项数量的标度律，并指出当模型类与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，低训练误差与弱泛化能力并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测通常需要学习非线性和时滞依赖关系，而NVAR/NG-RC模型是这类问题的典型模型。然而，该类模型的可辨识性（即训练误差的标度行为及其与泛化能力的关系）尚未被充分理解。

Method: 从理论角度分析NVAR/NG-RC模型的可辨识性，推导出训练误差随时间分辨率的（预）渐近标度律，区分特征库能否精确表示流映射（传播子）的早期Lie级数系数两种情况。针对多项式向量场控制的动力系统，采用单项式和傅里叶特征库进行验证，并考察时间分辨率、非线性度及延迟项数量的影响。

Result: 训练误差随时间分辨率的标度律依赖于特征库对流映射Lie级数系数的表示精度；延迟项降低最优单步训练误差，但仅在特征库提供足够非线性时才提升长时域预测性能；模型类与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，低训练误差与弱泛化能力共存。

Conclusion: NVAR/NG-RC模型的训练误差标度律为模型选择提供了理论指导，并强调了特征库设计对泛化能力的关键作用。数值实验在多个混沌动力系统上验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [190] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA存在过参数化问题，不同低秩因子对的条件数差异显著影响收敛速度。本文提出BaLoRA，通过将迭代投影到平衡流形上来改善损失景观的条件数，实现更快的收敛和更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然被广泛使用，但存在过参数化问题：多个低秩因子对可产生相同的适配权重矩阵，而这些因子对的条件数差异很大，导致收敛到不同损失极小值点时的收敛速度不同。作者希望通过改进条件数来加速LoRA的收敛。

Method: 提出BaLoRA（Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation），在LoRA优化过程中将迭代点投影到平衡流形上。该投影操作计算轻量，能改善损失景观的条件数，同时保持适配矩阵不变。

Result: 理论分析和实验均表明，BaLoRA比标准LoRA收敛更快，在多种微调任务上取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过平衡流形投影改善条件数是一种有效且轻量级的LoRA改进方案，能同时提升收敛速度和最终性能。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [191] [When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31504)
*Dylan Steiner,Gustavo Arango-Argoty,Gerald Sun,Etai Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECAT是一个模型无关的事后评估框架，用于区分多模态表示中的共享生物学、单模态生物学和虚假关联，通过五项零基准指标和规则决策程序对表示进行分类，并在合成数据（2500+表示）和真实TCGA数据（8979患者）上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态肿瘤模型虽然预测准确，但无法区分模型学到的是跨模态共享的生物学知识、单模态特有的生物学知识，还是反映混杂因素的虚假关联。现有方法缺乏对此进行系统诊断的工具。

Method: 提出DECAT框架，包含五个零基准指标和一个基于规则的决策程序，将多模态表示分为四个诊断场景。框架无需知道具体混杂因素，在证据不足时返回不确定结果。在合成数据（4种多模态模型类，2500+表示）和真实TCGA数据（8979患者）上验证。

Result: CLIP等纠缠模型在共享生物学检测上近乎完美，但在大多数不存在共享生物学的真实基础模型嵌入中错误声称存在共享生物学。这种错误率随混杂强度增加而上升。在TCGA嵌入和五个病理基础模型上，DECAT能检测到AUROC无法发现的混杂，且无需混杂标签。

Conclusion: DECAT能够有效诊断多模态表示中的共享生物学与虚假关联，揭示了纠缠模型在缺乏真正共享信号时容易被混杂因素误导的问题，为多模态模型的生物学可解释性提供了重要评估工具。

Abstract: Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.

</details>


### [192] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个包含37k+化合物、136种细胞背景、125万个转录组样本的跨数据集资源库，通过标准化处理实现跨数据集扰动转录组数据的整合与复用。研究发现在不同数据集间，相同化合物的logFC排名和幅度一致性较弱，但方向一致性较高。该资源作为预训练数据可提升化合物表示学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子转录组数据资源分散在不同技术平台、元数据标准、对照方式、剂量和预处理流程之间，缺乏统一整合的跨数据集资源，阻碍了大规模扰动模型的训练与评估。

Method: 整合多来源转录组数据，统一标识符、元数据和预处理流程，构建包含37k+化合物、136种细胞背景、125万样本、8种检测类型的标准化资源库；采用条件级别效应计算进行跨数据集一致性分析，并在化合物表示学习任务中评估预训练效果。

Result: 跨数据集相同化合物在logFC排名和幅度上一致性弱于同背景不同化合物的基线，但logFC方向一致性显著更稳定。基于Chem-PerturBridge的预训练嵌入在化合物留出评估和11个数据集的分子留出评估中，均优于或持平L1000嵌入、Morgan指纹等基线方法。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge可同时支持跨数据集信号一致性的诊断评估以及异质性扰动转录组数据的模型复用，为大规模化合物表示学习提供有效预训练资源。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [193] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动力系统重建中的动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap），即有限时域概率目标会损害动力学的准确性或使预测不确定性脱离局部切向动力学。作者提出KAFFEE框架，基于可微分扩展卡尔曼滤波，通过局部预测残差评估似然并传递协方差，有效缓解了核心坍缩、噪声掩蔽和盲目不确定性三种失效模式，在随机超混沌Lorenz-96和13个混沌系统上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动力系统重建方法在追求有限时域概率目标时，往往导致学习到的动力学退化，或者预测不确定性无法反映局部切向动力学，即存在动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap）。作者旨在识别这一差距的机制并提出缓解方法。

Method: 提出KAFFEE（Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation）框架，采用可微分扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）进行训练：在局部预测残差（新息）上评估似然，同时通过学习的局部雅可比矩阵传递协方差，从而保持动态与概率的一致性。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorentz-96上，KAFFEE减少了核心坍缩、噪声掩蔽和盲目不确定性三种失效模式，改善了动力学不变量的重建，同时保持了竞争性的预测得分。在13个混沌系统的DSR基础模型概率适配中，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波并基本保留零样本动力学。

Conclusion: 动态-概率一致性差距是动力系统重建中一个关键但被忽视的问题；KAFFEE框架通过将概率估计与局部切向动力学对齐，有效弥合了这一差距，为不确定性感知的动力系统重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [194] [Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31562)
*Kian Kenyon-Dean,Alina Selega,Ihab Bendidi,Jordan M. Sorokin,Luca Bertinetto,David Errington,Hayley Donnella,Oren Kraus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TxFM模型，通过精心设计的掩码自编码方法和高质量训练数据DiverseRNA-1.4M，在转录组学表示学习中超越大规模基础模型，验证了归纳式自监督学习的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转录组基础模型在基因表达建模中表现不如线性基线，引发疑问：深度表示学习是否比直接使用原始转录计数更具优势？本文旨在探究并解决这一问题。

Method: 提出TxFM模型，采用针对多样化RNA-seq计数数据定制的掩码自编码方法，通过消融研究确定关键架构配置，并构建DiverseRNA-1.4M训练语料库。

Result: TxFM在归纳式表示学习评估中表现优异，在DiverseRNA-1.4M上训练的模型超越了在规模大100倍以上的图谱级语料库上训练的基础模型。

Conclusion: 归纳式自监督学习是转录组学表征的可行建模方法，关键在于模型架构与训练数据质量的精心结合。

Abstract: RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.

</details>


### [195] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHARM模型，结合通道级文本描述与Transformer编码器，通过JEPA自监督学习和描述感知门控机制，实现异构多元时间序列的通用表示学习，在线性探测下达到强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的架构在语言和视觉领域推动了序列建模发展，但针对异构多元时间序列的通用表示学习仍未被充分探索。

Method: 引入CHARM模型，采用通道级文本描述结合通道顺序等变的Transformer编码器；使用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）训练，设计新型损失函数促进信息性且时间稳定的嵌入；通过潜在空间预测增强对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，描述感知门控提供可解释性。

Result: 在异常检测、分类、短期和长期预测任务中，仅使用线性探测即可达到强性能；性能主要由JEPA目标和条件架构驱动，文本描述作为跨数据集泛化的通道标识符。

Conclusion: CHARM通过融合通道文本描述与JEPA自监督学习，有效实现了异构多元时间序列的通用表示，兼顾了性能、鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [A Parameter-Free Restart Scheme with Only a Parallelizable $\log\log(1/ε)$ Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30502)
*Yue Wu,Benjamin Grimmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免参数的重启策略，可应用于任意一阶方法，通过并行实例通信避免已知缺陷（需问题先验知识、对数开销、初始预热），在亚线性加速场景下仅需O(log log(1/ε))个并行进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重启方案存在三个缺陷：需要不切实际的问题知识、在 oracle 复杂度中引入对数开销因子、以及需要非平凡的初始预热阶段。本文旨在消除这些缺陷。

Method: 提出一种免参数重启方法，动态部署一阶方法的并行实例，采用Renegar和Grimmer风格的进度通信机制，自动适应问题结构以加速收敛。

Result: 当加速率为亚线性时，仅需O(log log(1/ε))个并行进程，且避免了昂贵的预热阶段和问题先验知识需求。

Conclusion: 该方法为任意一阶方法提供了一种通用的、免参数的重启框架，在理论上消除了现有重启方案的主要缺陷。

Abstract: It is well-known that first-order methods can offer accelerated convergence rates in the presence of growth structures. Restarting schemes provide a general tool for such speed-ups. These schemes typically either require unrealistic problem knowledge, incur logarithmic overhead factors in oracle complexity, and/or have a nontrivial initial burn-in phase. We present a parameter-free approach for restarting any first-order method, avoiding these three drawbacks. Our approach dynamically deploys parallel instances of a given first-order method communicating progress in the style of Renegar and Grimmer. Our optimized scheme avoids expensive burn-ins and only requires $O(\log\log(1/ε))$ parallel processes when the accelerated rate is sublinear.

</details>


### [197] [Benchmarking Bilevel Derivative-Free Optimization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30531)
*Charles Audet,Valentin Dijon,Youssef Diouane*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双层无导数优化（BL-DFO）算法，提出了一种包含裁判程序的基准测试方法，用于严格验证解的可行性与计算成本公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BL-DFO基准测试技术未能严格验证解的可行性，也未充分考量上下层求解器的计算开销，导致算法比较不公平。

Method: 提出一种基准测试方法，引入名为"裁判程序"的后优化步骤以剔除不可行解，并将上下层求解器的计算代价纳入总体计算成本。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提基准测试方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为BL-DFO算法提供了更公平、严格的评估框架，解决了现有基准测试中解可行性与计算成本衡量的缺陷。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization involves an upper-level and a lower-level decision maker. The lower-level optimization problem is nested within the constraints of the upper-level one. A point is said to be admissible for the bilevel problem if it satisfies all constraints and is optimal for the lower-level decision-maker. Bilevel derivative-free optimization (BL-DFO) algorithms address bilevel optimization problems in which either the upper-level or the lower-level problem is solved using a derivative-free optimization method. In this context, existing BL-DFO benchmarking techniques often do not rigorously validate the admissibility of proposed solutions, and do not adequately account for the computational effort deployed by the upper- and lower-level solvers. This work proposes a benchmarking methodology for BL-DFO algorithms. A post-optimization procedure, named refereeing procedure, is introduced to discard non-admissible points and ensure a fair comparison between the algorithms. The computational effort deployed by upper- and lower-level solvers are also taken into account into the overall computational cost. Numerical experiments illustrate the benchmarking methodology.

</details>


### [198] [A characterization of maximal inhomogeneous-quadratic-free sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30602)
*Gonzalo Muñoz,Joseph Paat,Felipe Serrano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非齐次二次不等式定义集合S的极大S-自由集的特征，利用非扩张函数给出了完整刻画，与齐次情形的结果一起完整描述了所有极大二次自由集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整数规划中，极大晶格自由集已有明确刻画；而在二次优化中，Muñoz等人（2025）刻画了齐次二次不等式定义的极大S-自由集。本文旨在将该刻画推广至非齐次二次不等式情形，从而完整解决极大二次自由集的刻画问题。

Method: 采用相交割框架，利用非扩张函数作为核心工具，对由非齐次二次不等式定义的集合S，刻画其极大S-自由集的结构特征。

Result: 完整刻画了由非齐次二次不等式定义集合S的极大S-自由集，且该刻画基于非扩张函数构建。

Conclusion: 与齐次情形的结果相结合，本文完成了对每一个极大二次自由集通过非扩张函数的完整刻画。

Abstract: The intersection cut framework is a versatile tool for generating valid inequalities in optimization. Its main ingredients are so-called $S$-free sets: convex sets whose interiors do not intersect a given set $S$. Among these, inclusion-wise maximal $S$-free sets are particularly important, as they yield the strongest intersection cuts. In the integer programming setting, maximal lattice-free sets are well studied and admit explicit characterizations. In the quadratic optimization context, Muñoz, Paat, and Serrano (2025) characterized maximal $S$-free sets when $S$ is defined by a homogeneous quadratic inequality. In this work, we characterize maximal $S$-free sets when $S$ is defined by an inhomogeneous quadratic inequality. As in the homogeneous case, our characterization is built using non-expansive functions. Together with the results in the homogeneous case, our results complete a characterization of $every$ maximal quadratic-free set via non-expansive functions.

</details>


### [199] [BUP-TR: Bayesian Underdetermined Projection Trust-Region Methods for Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30841)
*Wei Hu,Pengcheng Xie,Ya-Xiang Yuan,Li Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出BUP-TR方法，用贝叶斯先验正则化替代经典的最小Frobenius范数补全规则，用于欠定二次插值模型构建。该方法通过MAP估计选择模型，并引入MAP-poisedness几何测试与修复程序，在标准假设下实现全局一阶收敛和O(ε^{-2})评估复杂度。BUP-NEWUOA实现相比NEWUOA等标准无导数优化器在固定评估预算下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无导数信赖域方法中，欠定二次插值模型存在歧义性，经典方法通过预设范数（如最小Frobenius Hessian更新）消除歧义。本文旨在用贝叶斯先验正则化替代这种预设规则，使模型选择更灵活且可结合先验知识。

Method: 将插值方程作为精确约束，在仿射族中选择最大后验（MAP）二次模型，等价于在先验精度矩阵定义的范数下将先验模型投影到插值仿射族上。同时利用该精度矩阵定义MAP-poisedness几何测试及修复程序。

Result: 在标准光滑性、几何和先验精度假设下，硬MAP模型具有完全线性性；信赖域方法达到全局一阶收敛，评估复杂度为O(ε^{-2})（含修复评估）。BUP-NEWUOA实现相比NEWUOA等标准求解器在固定评估预算下性能更优。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯先验正则化可有效替代经典范数补全规则用于无导数优化，在理论收敛保证和实际性能上均具优势。

Abstract: Underdetermined quadratic interpolation is a standard model-construction tool in model-based derivative-free trust-region methods: it keeps the number of function evaluations manageable, but leaves many quadratic models consistent with the sampled values. Classical solvers remove this ambiguity by prescribing a norm or a model-change measure, such as the least-Frobenius Hessian update in Powell-type methods. We propose BUP-TR (Bayesian Underdetermined Projection Trust-Region), which replaces this prescribed completion rule by a prior-regularized one. At each iteration, the interpolation equations are imposed as exact constraints, and the model is chosen as the maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) element of the affine family of interpolating quadratics, equivalently as the projection of a prior model onto this family in the norm defined by the precision matrix. The precision matrix used in this completion also defines a geometry test for the interpolation set, leading to MAP-poisedness and a corresponding repair procedure. Under standard smoothness, geometry, and prior-accuracy assumptions, the hard-MAP models are fully linear, and the resulting trust-region method attains global first-order convergence with O(epsilon^{-2}) evaluation complexity, including repair evaluations. A NEWUOA-style implementation, BUP-NEWUOA, improves performance under fixed evaluation budgets over NEWUOA and other standard derivative-free solvers on the test set considered.

</details>


### [200] [MATRO: Metric-Aware Fully Quadratic Trust Regions for Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30845)
*Wei Hu,Pengcheng Xie,Ya-Xiang Yuan,Li Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MATRO（度量感知信赖域优化），一种在信赖域方法中使用椭球形状（由Hessian诱导的曲率度量定义）替代传统欧几里得球的完全二次插值框架，以应对目标函数尺度不良或局部各向异性问题，并在标准假设下保持了O(n^2 epsilon^{-2})的一阶评价复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当黑箱目标函数尺度不良或局部各向异性时，欧几里得球信赖域会被最陡局部方向主导，沿慢变方向的进展受限。现有方法缺乏自适应度量来捕捉局部曲率信息以塑造信赖域形状。

Method: 两层结构：1) 对任意正定度量M_k，通过变换y=M_k^{1/2}s将椭球子问题转化为标准欧几里得信赖域子问题，在诱导坐标下统一度量契约；2) 度量从插值模型的Hessian选取：正定二次型时采用体积归一化曲率度量使诱导Hessian各向同性，不定Hessian时采用绝对曲率度量平衡曲率幅值并保留符号。

Result: 在标准完全二次假设和度量契约下，MATRO保持O(n^2 epsilon^{-2})的一阶评价复杂度。实验表明曲率形状信赖域在插值Hessian捕捉到稳定局部各向异性时最有效，但稠密线性代数成本在宽松精度或廉价解析测试中最明显。

Conclusion: 基于曲率的椭球信赖域能有效处理各向异性问题，其性能依赖于Hessian能否稳定捕捉局部各向异性结构，代价是额外的稠密线性代数计算开销。

Abstract: Model-based derivative-free trust-region methods build local models from function values and accept trial steps only where these models are expected to be reliable. This paper studies the shape of that trusted region. When a black-box objective is badly scaled or locally anisotropic, a Euclidean ball can be forced by the steepest local direction and can therefore restrict progress along directions of slow variation. We propose MATRO (Metric-Aware Trust-Region Optimization), a fully quadratic interpolation framework in which the trust region is the ellipsoid s^T M_k s <= Delta_k^2. The method is built in two layers. First, for any positive definite metric M_k, the induced variable y = M_k^{1/2} s converts the ellipsoidal subproblem into a standard Euclidean trust-region subproblem. Model decrease, ratio tests, radius updates, and fully quadratic interpolation theory can then be stated in induced coordinates under a uniform metric contract. Second, the metric is selected from the Hessian of the interpolation model. For positive definite quadratics, the volume-normalized curvature metric is the unique shape that makes the induced Hessian isotropic, and the corresponding metric trust-region step is a truncated Newton step. For indefinite fitted Hessians, an absolute-curvature metric balances curvature magnitudes while preserving the signs of the model curvature. Under the standard fully quadratic assumptions and the metric contract, MATRO retains the first-order evaluation-complexity order O(n^2 epsilon^{-2}) for full quadratic interpolation. Experiments on More-Wild benchmarks, controlled anisotropy tests, and two-dimensional trajectories show that curvature-shaped trust regions are most useful when the interpolation Hessian captures a stable local anisotropy, while their dense linear-algebra cost is most visible at loose accuracies or on inexpensive analytic tests.

</details>


### [201] [A General Recipe for Parameter-Free Nonconvex Optimization via Higher-Order Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30891)
*Naoki Marumo,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高阶正则化的系统框架，用于构建光滑非凸优化的无参数算法，无需回溯或接受测试即可获得最优复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑非凸优化中，现有算法通常需要手动调整正则化参数或依赖回溯等接受测试，缺乏一种系统性的无参数构造方法。

Method: 基于高阶正则化，每一步从正则化局部模型计算更新，且正则化指数超过模型误差阶数，使得算法对正则化参数的误设具有鲁棒性。

Result: 将框架应用于梯度下降、牛顿法、高斯-牛顿法、随机梯度下降和PAGE，得到的无参数算法在目标精度上实现了最优或已知最佳的复杂度界；在参数已知时，适当调参也能恢复最优依赖。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种通用的无参数算法设计范式，在无需先验知识的情况下达到最优复杂度，并可灵活适配参数已知场景。

Abstract: We develop a systematic framework for constructing parameter-free algorithms for smooth nonconvex optimization. The framework is based on higher-order regularization: each step is computed from a regularized local model whose regularization exponent exceeds the order of the model error. This design makes the resulting method robust to misspecification of the regularization parameter and yields complexity bounds without backtracking or other acceptance tests. We apply the framework to gradient descent, Newton's method, the Gauss--Newton method, stochastic gradient descent, and PAGE. Without prior knowledge of problem-dependent parameters, the resulting algorithms achieve complexity bounds with optimal or best-known dependence on the target accuracy. When the problem-dependent parameters are known up to constant factors, suitable tuning also recovers the optimal or best-known dependence on those parameters.

</details>


### [202] [A Unifying View of Anchoring via Operator-Side Tikhonov Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30905)
*Zihao Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的算子端视角来理解锚定方法：通过向基础方法的算子添加一个衰减的Tikhonov正则项，然后直接运行未修改的基础方法。这种构造可以复现Halpern迭代，并衍生出前向步、外梯度(EG)和过去外梯度(PEG)的正则化变体。在无约束单调Lipschitz设定下，四种方法共享一个残差递推关系，分别获得$O(1/k)$、$O(1/\sqrt{k})$和$O(1/k)$的残差范数收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的锚定变体虽然具有尖锐的最后迭代保证，但从更新层面来看，锚点的放置是算法特定的且概念上不透明。作者希望揭示锚定方法的统一本质，通过算子端的单一构造来理解并推广锚定技术。

Method: 提出统一构造：对基础方法查询的算子添加衰减的Tikhonov正则项（即向恒等算子方向拉动），然后运行未经修改的基础方法。具体实例化为：Picard迭代→Halpern迭代；前向步→正则化前向步；EG→正则化EG；PEG→正则化PEG。四种方法共享统一的残差递推分析框架。

Result: 在无约束单调Lipschitz设定下，正则化前向步获得$O(1/\sqrt{k})$残差收敛率；正则化EG和PEG获得$O(1/k)$残差收敛率，与Halpern迭代的最优率一致。此外，前向步实例化给出了新的残差收敛保证。

Conclusion: 锚定方法可以统一解释为算子端的Tikhonov正则化，这为设计新的锚定变体提供了系统性框架，且不同变体的锚点放置模式自然地继承了基础方法的查询模式。

Abstract: Anchored fixed point and monotone equation methods, including Halpern iteration, extra anchored gradient, and their relatives, add a vanishing pull toward a reference point to obtain last-iterate guarantees. Existing anchored variants often achieve sharp last-iterate guarantees, but from the update-level perspective the placement of the anchor can be algorithm-specific and conceptually opaque. We show that anchoring admits a single operator-side construction: regularize the operator queried by the base method with a vanishing Tikhonov term, then run the unmodified base method. Applied to the Picard iteration, this recipe reproduces the Halpern iteration; applied to the forward step, extragradient (EG), and past extragradient (PEG, also known as Popov's method), it yields three variants whose anchor placements inherit the base method's query pattern. The forward-step instantiation gives a new residual convergence guarantee, while the EG and PEG instantiations give new regularized variants. The four analyses share a residual recurrence, recovering the $O(1/k)$ Halpern residual-norm convergence rate, giving $O(1/\sqrt{k})$ for the regularized forward step, and giving $O(1/k)$ for the regularized EG and PEG variants in the unconstrained monotone Lipschitz setting.

</details>


### [203] [Bilevel optimization for smart irrigation control: a framework for enhancing water-use efficiency in agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31133)
*Roman Chertovskih,Nathalie Khalil,Luís Sousa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对农业水资源短缺问题，提出基于双层优化算法的智能灌溉控制系统，上层中心控制器管理每日用水限额和田间分配，下层集中调度器确定跨田协作灌溉方案，在数字仿真中显著提升水资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖加剧水资源短缺，农业灌溉季节性需求导致大量浪费，亟需高效的用水管理方案。

Method: 构建双层优化框架：上层作为中心控制器管理每日用水限额及田间分配，下层作为集中调度器确定最优协作灌溉执行方案，在满足全局资源约束的同时兼顾局部灌溉需求。

Result: 数字仿真工具初步结果表明，所提框架显著提升了水资源利用效率，尤其在供水受限场景下支持可持续灌溉实践。

Conclusion: 提出的双层优化智能灌溉控制系统能有效提高农业用水效率，在缺水条件下具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Global warming has intensified water scarcity, posing a critical challenge for the agricultural sector, where seasonal demand often leads to significant wastage. This work addresses the need for efficient water management by proposing a smart irrigation control system based on a bilevel optimization algorithm. The framework aims to minimize water consumption across multiple fields simultaneously or, under supply constraints, to distribute available water equitably to minimize deviations from required levels. To achieve this, the problem is modeled hierarchically: the upper level acts as a central controller managing daily water limits and field-wise allocation, while the lower level acts as a centralized scheduler determining the optimal cooperative irrigation execution across all fields. This structure ensures that both global resource constraints and local irrigation needs are met efficiently. Preliminary results from digital simulation tools suggest that the proposed framework significantly improves water-use efficiency and supports sustainable irrigation practices, particularly in water-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [204] [Sum-of-squares certificates for symmetric polynomials on the hypercube: a counterexample to a conjecture of De Klerk and Laurent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31169)
*Sven Polak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了超立方体上非负多项式的平方和表示，通过对称性约化、稀疏性约化、有理取整和精确验证等方法，证明了对n=8时De Klerk和Laurent猜想的常数值C_8≤11/1000<1/80，从而否定了该猜想在n=8时的最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证De Klerk和Laurent关于超立方体上多项式x_1...x_n+C_n属于截断二次模M_{n,n}的最小常数C_n的猜想，特别是针对n=8的情况。

Method: 将对称性约化技术特化到超立方体（生成元x_i-x_i^2虽非对称群不变但构成不变集），结合启发式稀疏性约化、有理取整步骤以及有理数域上的精确验证。

Result: 证明了C_8≤11/1000<1/80，否定了原猜想在n=8时的最优值C_n=1/(n(n+2))。

Conclusion: 通过组合多种约化与验证技术，成功否定了De Klerk和Laurent关于n=8时的猜想，表明该猜想在一般偶数n下可能不成立。

Abstract: This paper studies sum-of-squares (SoS) representations of nonnegative polynomials over the hypercube $[0,1]^n$. De Klerk and Laurent (SIAM J. Optim., 2010) conjectured that the smallest constant $C_n$ such that the polynomial $x_1\cdots x_n +C_n$ is contained in the degree-$n$ truncated quadratic module $M_{n,n}(x_1-x_1^2,\ldots,x_n-x_n^2)$ of the hypercube is $C_n=1/(n(n+2))$, for $n$ even. We specialize symmetry reduction techniques for finding sum-of-squares certificates to the hypercube, where the generators $x_i-x_i^2$ are not individually invariant under the symmetric group but form an invariant set, and apply them to this conjecture. Combining this reduction with a further (heuristic) sparsity reduction, a rational rounding step, and an exact verification over $\mathbb{Q}$, we prove the bound $C_8\leq 11/1000 <1/80$. In particular, this disproves the conjectured optimal value for $n=8$.

</details>


### [205] [Posterior and Likelihood Sensitivity in Bayesian Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31306)
*Jun-ya Gotoh,Andrew E. B. Lim,Michael Jong Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了最坏情况后验敏感性和最坏情况似然敏感性两个概念，分别衡量贝叶斯模型中后验分布和似然函数的最坏情况扰动对期望成本的影响。后验敏感性在参数不确定性通过学习消除时趋于零，但似然敏感性不会消失。通过分布鲁棒优化可在性能（期望成本）与鲁棒性（后验和似然敏感性）之间实现近乎帕累托最优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者对模型假设偏离所导致的样本外期望成本敏感性存在担忧，需要一种量化鲁棒性的方法，以评估后验分布和似然函数在遭受最坏情况扰动时对决策的影响。

Method: 定义了最坏情况后验敏感性和最坏情况似然敏感性，基于偏差度量构建不确定性集合，并采用分布鲁棒优化框架来权衡性能与鲁棒性。

Result: 后验敏感性在参数学习消除不确定性时趋于零，但似然敏感性不会随参数学习而消失。分布鲁棒优化可以实现性能与鲁棒性的近乎帕累托最优权衡。

Conclusion: 决策者应同时关注后验敏感性和似然敏感性，前者可通过充分学习消除，后者则是贝叶斯模型内在的鲁棒性挑战，需通过分布鲁棒优化进行权衡。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of worst-case posterior and worst-case likelihood sensitivity. These measure, respectively, the sensitivity of the expected cost to worst-case perturbations of the posterior distribution and worst-case perturbations of the likelihood of a Bayesian model. Each defines a quantitative measure of robustness. A decision maker concerned about the sensitivity of the out-of-sample expected cost to deviations from her assumptions will want a decision for which both sensitivities are small. We derive posterior and likelihood sensitivities for uncertainty sets defined in terms of deviation measures. Posterior sensitivity vanishes when the posterior variance shrinks to zero, which occurs when parameter uncertainty is eliminated from learning. Parameter learning does not eliminate likelihood sensitivity. A distributionally robust formulation of a Bayesian optimization problem makes a near-Pareto-optimal tradeoff between performance (expected cost) and robustness (posterior and likelihood sensitivity).

</details>


### [206] [Wall-Clock Complexity for Zeroth-Order Optimization with Tunable Oracle Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31346)
*Alexandra Suvorikova,Igor Pavlov,Artem Vasin,Georgii Bychkov,Anastasia Antsiferova,Darina Dvinskikh,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究精度感知的零阶（黑箱）优化问题，其中查询精度δ与成本c(δ)相关，目标是在满足精度约束下最小化总时间。文章分析了不同优化方案（包括加速与非加速方法）在墙钟时间下的表现，发现加速方法可能不如非加速方法，并给出了常数精度策略最优的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在梯度不可用、目标函数依赖昂贵仿真的黑箱优化场景中，oracle精度是可调的：高精度查询降低噪声但计算成本更高。现有研究缺乏一个统一框架来刻画这种精度-成本权衡对优化总时间的影响。

Method: 构建精度感知的墙钟模型，总时间T_total = Σc(δ_k)，考虑oracle类型、噪声模型和优化方案的相互作用，推导出算法参数在墙钟时间下的最优选择。

Result: 加速方法在墙钟时间下可能劣于非加速方案；给出了常数精度策略在Big-O意义下最优的条件。

Conclusion: 提供了一个统一的方法论，将收敛保证转化为实际的精度选择和批处理建议，帮助 practitioners 在精度与计算成本之间做出最优权衡。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (black-box) optimization is applied when gradients are unavailable and objective evaluations rely on expensive simulations. In many such applications, the oracle fidelity is tunable: higher-accuracy queries reduce noise but incur higher computational costs. To capture this trade-off, we study an accuracy-aware wall-clock model where each query with fidelity $δ$ has a cost $c(δ)$, and we minimize the total time $T_{\mathrm{total}} = \sum_{k=1}^{N} c(δ_k)$, subject to a target accuracy constraint. We show how the choice of oracle type, noise model, and optimization scheme induces explicit wall-clock-optimal choices for the algorithmic parameters. For instance, we demonstrate that accelerated methods can be wall-clock inferior to non-accelerated schemes. Furthermore, we characterize the conditions under which a constant fidelity strategy is optimal in the Big-O sense. Our framework provides a unified methodology to translate convergence guarantees into practical fidelity and batching recommendations.

</details>


### [207] [Stepsize Hedging: an Alternative Mechanism for Accelerating Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31386)
*Jason M. Altschuler,Pablo A. Parrilo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一个令人惊讶的发现：仅通过选择更好的步长（stepsize hedging），就可以加速梯度下降方法，无需修改算法核心结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示步长选择对梯度下降收敛速度的重要影响，证明通过步长"对冲"策略即可实现加速，为优化算法提供新的理解视角。

Method: 采用综述性论述方式，介绍了步长对冲（stepsize hedging）这一概念及其对梯度下降加速的理论解释。

Result: 表明仅通过精心设计的步长调度策略，即可显著提升梯度下降的收敛速度，实现类似动量法或自适应方法的加速效果。

Conclusion: 步长对冲是一种被低估的加速策略，为理解和改进梯度下降算法提供了简洁而有力的思路。

Abstract: Can gradient descent be accelerated by just choosing better stepsizes? Surprisingly, the answer is yes. This short expository article provides an accessible introduction to this phenomenon of stepsize hedging.

</details>


### [208] [Mixed-Precision GPU Acceleration for Large-Scale Minimum Enclosing Ball Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31425)
*Ling Liang,Lei Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对球集合的最小包围球问题，开发了一种混合精度GPU优化框架。该框架基于二阶锥规划重构和相对型不精确近端增广拉格朗日方法(ripALM)，通过混合精度缩减策略（低精度筛选+高精度精化+后验可行性检查）在GPU上高效并行计算，在大规模实例上显著快于CPU几何算法和通用锥规划求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球集合的最小包围球问题在大规模场景下计算成本高，现有CPU几何算法和通用锥规划求解器效率不足，亟需利用GPU并行计算能力加速。

Method: 1) 将最小包围球问题等价重构为二阶锥规划问题；2) 采用相对型不精确近端增广拉格朗日方法(ripALM)，内部子问题仅需求解至与进度相关的相对精度；3) 利用近端增广拉格朗日的约束可分结构，将目标函数、梯度、广义Hessian和对偶乘子更新并行映射到GPU；4) 提出混合精度缩减策略：低精度ripALM筛选边界附近球体，高精度ripALM精化缩减后问题，后验可行性检查检测并重新引入被错误丢弃的球体。

Result: ripALM及混合精度ripALM在大规模实例上实现了高精度，计算速度显著快于CPU几何算法和通用锥规划求解器。

Conclusion: 混合精度GPU优化框架有效解决了大规模球集合的最小包围球问题，兼顾了高精度和高效率，验证了低精度用于筛选和热启动、高精度用于最终可行性的混合策略的有效性。

Abstract: A mixed-precision GPU-oriented optimization framework is developed for computing the minimum enclosing ball of a collection of balls. The approach is built on an equivalent second-order cone programming reformulation and a relative-type inexact proximal augmented Lagrangian method (ripALM), which provides a high-accuracy optimization backbone while solving the inner subproblems only to a progress-dependent relative accuracy. The proximal augmented Lagrangian inherits a constraint-wise separable structure: its objective, gradient, generalized Hessian, and multiplier updates can be efficiently evaluated on GPUs as parallel maps over the input balls followed by reductions. To further improve efficiency, a mixed-precision reduction strategy is introduced. A low-precision ripALM run identifies balls near the approximate boundary, a high-precision ripALM run refines the reduced problem, and a full a posteriori feasibility check detects and reintroduces any violated discarded balls. Thus, low precision is used only for screening and warm starting, while the final feasibility is enforced against the original problem. Numerical experiments show that ripALM and mixed-precision ripALM achieve high accuracy and are substantially faster than the tested CPU-based geometric software and general-purpose conic solvers on large-scale instances.

</details>


### [209] [Squared polynomial approximation kernels for the hypercube: improved error bounds and implications for Lasserre hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31496)
*Sander Gribling,Etienne de Klerk,Juan C. Vera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一族新的多项式近似核，用于在超立方体 $[-1,1]^n$ 上近似非负多项式，实现了系数 $\ell_1$ 范数下 $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$ 的误差界，将已知的 $O(1/r)$ 界显著改进。作为推论，Lasserre 层次在超立方体多项式优化上的收敛率也从 $O(1/r)$ 改进到 $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进超立方体上非负多项式近似核的误差界，从而提升 Lasserre 层次在多项式优化问题中的收敛速率。

Method: 构造一族新的多项式平方和核，通过优化系数 $\ell_1$ 范数下的近似误差来实现 $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$ 的改进界。

Result: 新核在系数 $\ell_1$ 范数误差上达到 $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$，优于已知的 $O(1/r)$；Lasserre 层次收敛率相应提升。

Conclusion: 提出一族更优的多项式平方和近似核，显著改进了超立方体上多项式优化中 Lasserre 层次的理论收敛速率。

Abstract: We propose a new family of polynomial approximation kernels for approximating nonnegative polynomials on the hypercube $[-1,1]^n$. Our Kernels produce polynomial sums-of-squares of degree $r$, achieving an $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$ error in the $\ell_1$-norm of the coefficients. This improves on the known error bound $O(1/r)$ from the literature. As a corollary, we obtain an improved convergence rate for the Lasserre hierarchy for polynomial optimization on the hypercube, again improving a known rate by Baldi and Slot from $O(1/r)$ to $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$.

</details>


### [210] [The sketched landing method for large-scale optimization under orthogonality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31505)
*Florentin Goyens,Simon Mataigne,Pierre-Antoine Absil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机草图的着陆方法变体，用于求解正交约束优化问题，通过低维随机草图矩阵降低每次迭代的计算成本，同时保持期望收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低着陆方法在每次迭代中计算法向分量（减少不可行性）和切向分量（降低目标函数）的高计算成本。

Method: 引入低维随机草图矩阵（包括密集高斯草图和稀疏子采样草图），分别用于近似法向分量和切向分量的计算，从而降低每次迭代的计算开销。

Result: 密集和稀疏两种草图矩阵均能有效降低单次迭代成本，并在期望意义下保持收敛保证。

Conclusion: 随机草图化是降低着陆方法计算负担的有效策略，且不影响其理论收敛性质。

Abstract: We propose the \emph{sketched landing method}, a randomized variant of the landing method for optimization under orthogonality constraints. Each landing step consists of the sum of a \emph{normal} component, which reduces infeasibility, and a \emph{tangent} component, which decreases the objective function. Our main contribution is the introduction of low-dimensional random \emph{sketch matrices} to reduce the computational cost of these directions. We consider both dense (Gaussian) and sparse (subsampling) sketch matrices, and show how they reduce the per-iteration cost while preserving convergence guarantees in expectation.

</details>


### [211] [A derivative-free particle method for optimization in Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31565)
*Hui Huang,Hicham Kouhkouh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类在可分希尔伯特空间中的随机交互粒子系统及其对应的平均场形式，扩展了基于共识的优化方法至无限维优化问题，建立了动力学的适定性、共识机制分析，并在适当假设下证明了长期时间内动力学向目标函数最小化器的收敛性，同时提出了面向数值实现的有限粒子系统算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典共识优化方法从有限维空间推广到无限维（可分希尔伯特空间）的优化问题，解决现有方法在无限维情形下缺乏理论保证和应用局限性的问题。

Method: 提出随机交互粒子系统及其平均场表述，保留共识驱动的结构特征，分析无限维动力学的适定性、共识机制，并在目标泛函的适当假设下推导长期收敛保证，同时研究有限粒子系统的数值实现算法。

Result: 证明了所提动力学在无限维空间中的适定性，分析了共识机制，并在目标泛函的适当假设下建立了向最小化器收敛的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该工作将共识优化方法的应用范围成功扩展至一类广泛的无限维优化问题，为相关数值方法提供了理论依据和实用算法。

Abstract: We introduce a stochastic interacting particle system in separable Hilbert spaces together with its associated mean-field formulation. The model is shown to retain the characteristic consensus-driven structure of classical Consensus-Based Optimization, while accounting for the analytical challenges of infinite-dimensional dynamics. We establish well-posedness of the proposed dynamics and analyze the associated consensus mechanism. Furthermore, we derive convergence guarantees under suitable assumptions on the objective functional, showing concentration of the dynamics toward the minimizer in the long-time regime. This extends the applicability of the method to a broad class of infinite-dimensional optimization problems. In addition, we study the corresponding finite-particle system relevant for numerical implementation and propose a practical algorithm.

</details>


### [212] [The Value of Temporary Control for the M/M/1 Queue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31573)
*Odysseas Kanavetas,Camiel M. P. Koopmans,Floske M. Spieksma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究M/M/1队列中一次性临时服务速率控制选项，在指数分布时段内提供两种服务速率，目标是最小化持有成本与服务成本之和。通过值迭代算法近似期望总节省成本，并给出最优策略结构和强Blackwell最优性的理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在队列系统中，服务速率的动态调整可以降低运营成本，但实际中服务速率控制可能只能短暂启用。本文旨在探索一次性临时速率控制选项的价值，即在有限时间内启用额外服务速率，以平衡持有成本和服务成本。

Method: 采用M/M/1队列模型，引入一次性临时服务速率控制选项（指数分布时长），利用值迭代算法结合M-均匀几何递推方法，近似计算期望总节省成本（包括贴现和非贴现情形），并从理论上分析最优策略结构和强Blackwell最优性。

Result: 提出了基于值迭代的近似计算方法，能够有效估计期望总节省成本和期望总节省贴现未来成本；获得了最优策略的结构性特征，并证明了强Blackwell最优性；数值实验验证了方法在不同模型实例中的有效性。

Conclusion: 一次性临时服务速率控制选项能够有效降低M/M/1队列系统的总成本，所提出的近似算法具有良好的收敛性和实用性，理论结果（最优策略结构、强Blackwell最优性）为实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this article, a one-off option for temporary service rate control for the M/M/1 queue is considered.
  After taking this option, during a single exponentially distributed period, two service rates are available for use. Once service rate control is lost, the system continues with a fixed service rate $μ$. The objective is to minimise the sum of holding costs and service costs.
  We approximate the expected total saved cost by taking the one-off option, depending on the starting state or starting distribution.
  Using the Value Iteration algorithm with $M$-uniform geometric recurrence, we present methods to approximate the expected total saved future cost, as well as the expected total saved discounted future cost. Furthermore, we obtain theoretical results on the structure of optimal policies and strong Blackwell optimality. The paper is concluded by numerically applying the methods to various instances of the model.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen是一个神经符号学流水线，用于从文本生成符合物理规律的物理学图表。它通过大语言模型提取场景图，用确定性求解器转换为精确几何图元，再通过细化的视觉模型进行迭代校正。在包含力学、光学和电磁学1,449个问题的基准测试中，显著优于GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 3 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型在生成物理图表时会系统地出现幻觉（如虚构力矢量）、忽略守恒定律并违反几何约束，无法保证物理准确性。

Method: 提出PhyDrawGen神经符号学流水线：(1) LLM从问题文本提取带类型的场景图；(2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图(PSLG)，编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑为精确几何基元；(3) 细化的Qwen-VL模型执行视觉化的propose-verify循环，迭代修正违反约束的情况。

Result: 在涵盖力学、光学和电磁学的1,449个问题基准上，PhyDrawGen显著优于GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 3 Pro，在异常对象问题上也表现出鲁棒的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 将符号约束求解与神经视觉验证相结合，是确保物理图表生成中严格遵守物理定律的有效途径。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [214] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出具身AI中基于观测预测的世界模型虽然在视觉上合理，但常常产生物理上错误的推演结果。作者提出应构建“物理可行”的世界模型——即识别并建模干预查询所需的最简物理抽象，通过模块化组件（环境表示、潜状态估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询响应）和自主编排器实现可解释、可验证的物理推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测预测型世界模型在视觉上看似合理，但在物理层面存在结构性缺陷：不同的物理系统可能在视觉上完全相同，但在干预下却产生不同结果。这种失败是结构性的，导致模型推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为。因此需要从物理可行性的角度重新设计世界模型。

Method: 提出物理可行的世界模型框架，核心思想是找到足以回答干预查询的最简物理抽象。模型由模块化组件构成：环境表示、潜状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应。自主编排器负责识别相关抽象并动态组合学习组件与结构化组件。过渡模型可以是解析的、模拟的、学习的或混合的，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过构建固定可见场景而改变潜物理参数的对照基准，暴露了现有模型的物理失败。并展示了该方法在现有系统无法正确回答的查询上的有效性，证明了正确的抽象不是最详细的世界模型，而是保留查询相关区分的最简模型。

Conclusion: 物理可行的世界模型应追求查询所需的最简物理抽象，而非最大细节的模拟。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计。为设计新世界模型提供了原则，为评估现有模型提供了可行性测试，并展示了编排器如何动态组装和适配物理可行模型用于规划、控制与验证。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [215] [Transforming and Encoding FTS for SAT Solving: What Helps, What Hurts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30563)
*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将因子化任务（factored tasks）编码为SAT问题，提出了多种将因子化转移关系转化为命题逻辑的编码策略，并分析了不同层次的并行性利用以及常见任务变换对SAT规划器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化任务作为一种比STRIPS和SAS+更紧凑的规划表示形式，现有方法仅局限于启发式搜索，缺乏基于SAT的规划方法，因此需要探索如何将因子化任务高效编码为SAT问题。

Method: 提出了多种将因子化转移关系翻译为命题逻辑的编码方案，从不同策略入手进行转换，同时分析在SAT框架下利用不同层次并行性的方式，并研究常见任务变换对SAT规划器性能的影响。

Result: 给出了将因子化任务编码为SAT问题的多种可行方案，并揭示了不同编码策略、并行性利用方式以及任务变换对SAT规划性能的影响。

Conclusion: 因子化任务可以有效地编码为SAT问题，且不同编码策略和并行性利用方式对规划性能有显著影响，为基于SAT的因子化任务规划奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [216] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究使用MAP-Elites（一种质量多样性算法）为第一人称射击（FPS）游戏设计关卡，提出了两种新的地图表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），并通过拓扑和涌现属性指标评估，结果表明新表示方法能生成比传统方法更具多样性和更高质量的FPS地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPS地图生成方法缺乏多样性与质量兼顾的优化框架，且传统地图表示方法（All-Black和Grid-Graph）对FPS地图的特征刻画能力有限。

Method: 采用MAP-Elits with Sliding Boundaries (MESB)算法，引入两种新颖地图表示（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），定义拓扑属性（仅依赖布局）和涌现属性（需实际游戏评估）两类指标，通过特征分析指导照明过程。

Result: 新的地图表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout）相比传统表示方法，能够生成更高多样性和更高质量的FPS地图。

Conclusion: 所提出的新型地图表示方法能有效提升FPS地图生成的多样性和质量，为质量多样性算法在游戏关卡设计中的应用提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate the application of MAP-Elites (a well-known quality diversity algorithm) to design levels for First-Person Shooter (FPS) games. We consider two well-known map representations (All-Black and Grid-Graph) and introduce two novel representations (Point-Line and Spatial-Layout) that improve the characterization of FPS maps. We define a series of metrics to describe maps' topological properties (which solely depend on maps' layout), and emergent properties (which must be evaluated through actual gameplay). We perform an in-depth analysis to identify the most suitable features to guide MAP-Elites illumination process. We apply MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB) to evolve populations of FPS maps. Our results show that the new representations can generate maps with higher diversity and quality than the representations previously used for evolving FPS maps.

</details>


### [217] [Uncertainty-Aware and Temporally Regulated Expert Advice in Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30576)
*Ahmed Abouelazm,Felix Klingebiel,Philip Schörner,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的探索框架，通过专家建议引导、自适应阈值触发、承诺冷却策略和随机早停启发式，在CARLA无信号交叉口导航任务中，将成功率提升5-7%并减少故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的探索行为对自动驾驶本质不安全——智能体必须体验新行为来学习，但探索可能导致碰撞或偏离道路，需要一种既能保证探索效率又能确保安全的方法。

Method: 1) 基于滚动缓冲区的自适应不确定性阈值触发专家建议；2) 承诺-冷却策略（commitment-cooldown）配合随机早停启发式控制引导时长和频率；3) 在离线IQN框架内共享经验回放缓冲区，融合专家轨迹与智能体经验。

Result: 在CARLA无信号交叉口导航任务中，相比IQN基线提升5-7%成功率，同时减少故障次数。

Conclusion: 风险敏感的不确定性度量与受调控的专家集成相结合，能够实现更安全、更高效的基于传感器的RL策略探索学习。

Abstract: Exploration in reinforcement learning for autonomous driving is inherently unsafe: agents must experience novel behaviors to learn, yet exploration can lead to collisions or off-road driving. We propose an uncertainty-aware framework that leverages expert advice to guide exploration while avoiding long-term dependence. Advice is triggered when epistemic or aleatoric uncertainty exceeds adaptive thresholds derived from rolling buffers, ensuring advice evolves with the agent's confidence. A commitment-cooldown strategy with a stochastic early-stop heuristic regulates the duration and frequency of guidance, exposing the agent to coherent maneuvers without exhausting the advice budget. Expert and agent experiences are combined in a shared replay buffer within an off-policy implicit quantile network (IQN) backbone, enabling efficient reuse of expert trajectories. Experiments in CARLA show that our method outperforms the IQN baseline, improving success by 5-7% and reducing failures, demonstrating that risk-sensitive uncertainty coupled with regulated expert integration enables safer and more efficient exploration for sensor-based RL policy learning in unsignalized intersection navigation.

</details>


### [218] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie,Keqi Han,Yunpeng Xiao,Hejie Cui,Guanchen Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Kai Shu,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHRBench是一个通过EHR-LLM-KB自动化流水线构建的近百万问答对的临床决策基准，用于评估LLM在诊断、治疗和预后三大核心任务上的实际临床决策能力，实验表明现有LLM在临床可靠性方面仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在真实临床决策任务上的可靠性尚未被充分理解，缺乏一个既具备规模化又保证质量的自动化医学决策基准来系统评估LLM的临床决策能力。

Method: 提出EHR-LLM-KB交互流水线：利用专用LLM自动将就诊级EHR轨迹转换为结构化模板并实例化为QA条目，同时利用基于知识库的系统性验证与丰富化来过滤幻觉或模糊关系，最终构建涵盖诊断、治疗、预后三类任务的近百万QA基准。

Result: 构建了960,067个QA条目，对30余个代表性LLM进行了基准测试，结果展示了不同设置下一致的能力趋势，验证了EHRBench的可靠性并揭示了LLM在临床可靠性方面的可操作差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench作为大规模、自动化的EHR临床决策基准，能够有效评估LLM在真实临床决策任务中的表现，并指出了当前LLM系统在临床可靠性方面仍需改进的方向。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [219] [Healthcare Mechanisms from Policy-as-Code Search under Strategic Provider Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30680)
*Zihan Wang,Xiang Xu,Hongyuan Zha,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种将医院机制设计重铸为语言模型程序合成的方法，通过多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim评估规则程序，并利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索合成可检查的混合目标程序，消除编码欺诈、减少拒绝并保留大部分利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准固定了提供者响应，无法评估机制所产生的均衡结果，因此需要一种能捕捉策略性提供者行为响应的机制评估方法。

Method: 将医院机制设计形式化为带类型的可检查规则程序，通过多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim（含五种策略性提供者渠道：编码、选择、延迟、努力、分诊）执行并评分；利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索在规则程序空间中合成最优程序。

Result: 激励扫描复现了经典卫生经济学发现（利润压力下的编码升级、低复杂度患者选择、古德哈特漂移）；单一审计杠杆暴露压力迁移（关闭编码渠道使低复杂度选择翻倍）；合成程序消除了编码升级，将拒绝率减半，并保留了大部分基线利润。

Conclusion: 可检查规则程序结合多智能体模拟与LLM引导搜索，能有效设计和评估策略性提供者环境下的医疗机制。

Abstract: Healthcare mechanisms are inseparable from the strategic provider response they induce: existing healthcare AI benchmarks hold this response fixed and so cannot evaluate mechanisms by the equilibrium they produce. We recast hospital mechanism design as program synthesis for language models: typed, inspectable rule programs are executed and scored by Medi-Sim, a multi-agent simulator with five strategic provider channels (coding, selection, delay, effort, triage). An incentive sweep recovers classical health-economics findings as adjacent regimes -- up-coding and low-complexity-patient selection under profit pressure, and Goodhart-style drift where measured performance becomes anti-correlated with true outcomes -- and a single audit lever exposes pressure migration: closing the coding channel more than doubles low-complexity selection. LLM-guided evolutionary code search over the same rule-program space then synthesizes an inspectable mixed-objective program that eliminates up-coding, halves rejection, and retains most of the profit-oriented baseline's funds.

</details>


### [220] [Generating Graph-like Rules for Knowledge Graph Reasoning via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30747)
*Haoxiang Cheng,Yunfei Wang,Chao Chen,Kewei Cheng,Zhipeng Lin,Haoxuan Li,Changjun Fan,Shixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRiD是一个将图状规则发现重构为离散生成过程的框架，通过两阶段训练（监督预训练+强化学习微调）解决传统规则挖掘方法忽略图状结构及生成模型无法直接应用于规则挖掘的问题，在KG补全任务上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规则挖掘方法主要关注简单链式规则，忽略了图中更丰富的结构信息（如环、分支）；同时，扩散模型等生成方法因训练目标与规则质量不匹配、非可微的规则质量指标无法直接优化模型而难以应用于规则挖掘。

Method: 提出GRiD框架，将图状规则发现重构为以目标关系为条件的离散生成过程。采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段监督预训练，从KG元图采样子图捕获结构先验；第二阶段强化学习，通过策略梯度优化直接由非可微规则质量指标引导微调。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，GRiD在KG补全任务中取得竞争性性能；消融研究证实了其效率和鲁棒性，并表明图状规则与链式规则在KG补全中具有互补性。

Conclusion: GRiD通过将图状规则发现转化为离散生成过程，结合监督预训练和强化学习微调，成功捕获了图结构中的丰富关系信息，在KG推理中具有有效性和互补性。

Abstract: Logical rules constitute a cornerstone of knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, valued for their interpretability and ability to model relational patterns. However, existing rule mining methods predominantly focus on simple chain-like rules and therefore neglect the richer relational information encoded in graph-like structures, such as cycles and branches. This limitation is further exacerbated by computational bottlenecks caused by the combinatorial explosion of the search space, which is especially challenging for graph-like rules. Meanwhile, generative approaches such as diffusion models, despite their success in other domains, can not be directly applied to rule mining because their training objectives are not aligned with the goal of learning high-quality rules, and non-differentiable KG rule quality metrics cannot directly guide model optimization. To address these limitations, we propose GRiD, a framework that reformulates graph-like rule discovery as a discrete generative process conditioned on the target relation. GRiD employs a two-phase training strategy. First, supervised pre-training enables GRiD to capture structural priors from subgraphs sampled from the KG meta-graph. Subsequently, reinforcement learning is applied to fine-tune GRiD through policy gradient optimization guided directly by non-differentiable rule-quality metrics. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that GRiD achieves competitive performance on KG completion tasks. Ablation studies confirm the efficiency and robustness of GRiD and further show that graph-like rules complement chain-like rules in KG completion. Our codes and datasets are available in https://github.com/Haoxiang-Cheng/GRiD

</details>


### [221] [Learning Agent-Compatible Context Management for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30785)
*Lu Yi,Runlin Lei,Liuyi Yao,Yuexiang Xie,Yuyang Li,Wenhao Zhang,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AdaCoM，通过外部LLM和强化学习为冻结的智能体自适应管理上下文，在长周期任务中显著提升性能，并发现保真度-可靠性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长周期任务中面临上下文累积导致的性能退化问题，现有方法需要训练智能体自身进行上下文管理，这限制了闭源智能体的适用性，且忽略了不同智能体需要不同管理策略的事实。

Method: 训练一个外部LLM（而非智能体本身）通过灵活的修改操作和端到端强化学习来管理冻结智能体的上下文，实现自适应上下文管理。

Result: 在多种智能体和基准测试上，AdaCoM通过保留任务约束和进展、剪除过期内容显著提升了性能；发现了保真度-可靠性权衡：高基线性能的智能体受益于高保真上下文保留，低性能智能体需要更激进的压缩。

Conclusion: AdaCoM在不同智能体间泛化有效，尤其适用于能力相似的智能体，为构建可复用的上下文管理器提供了实用路径。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly face long-horizon tasks such as web search and deep research in real-world applications, where accumulated context can cause long-context degradation and reasoning failures. Prior work mitigates this through context management with agent-side context control or fixed strategies such as summarization, which require training the agent itself for adaptation - making it impractical for closed-source agents and ignoring that different agents may require different strategies. We introduce Adaptive Context Management (AdaCoM), which trains an external LLM to manage the context of a frozen agent through flexible modification actions and end-to-end reinforcement learning. Across diverse agents on web search and deep research benchmarks, AdaCoM substantially improves performance by preserving task constraints and progress while pruning stale content. The learned strategies reveal a Fidelity-Reliability Trade-off: agents with higher vanilla ReAct performance benefit from higher-fidelity context preservation, whereas lower-performing agents require more aggressive compression to stay within a reliable reasoning regime. Transfer experiments show that AdaCoM generalizes most effectively across agents with similar capability (measured by vanilla ReAct performance), suggesting a practical path toward reusable context managers for agent systems.

</details>


### [222] [PReMISE: Policy Rubrics as Measurement Specifications for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30803)
*Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Tharindu Kumarage,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Anna Rumshisky,Pradeep Natarajan,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PReMISE框架，基于成对人类偏好数据发现策略级评估准则集，并从结构充分性、可靠性、偏好拟合和对抗鲁棒性四个维度审计LLM评估准则。研究发现没有任何原始准则源同时满足可靠、偏好预测和对抗鲁棒；PReMISE是唯一在适用性、特异性和有效维度上同时取得非平凡结果的准则源。通过偏好排名选择和可靠性约束修复操作，分别提升了评判准确率和降低了对抗利用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器的评分高度依赖于评估准则（rubrics），模糊的准则会导致模型奖励看似合理但编造事实或违背用户意图的回答。现有研究缺乏系统化的框架来发现和审计高质量的评估准则集。

Method: 提出PReMISE框架：(i) 基于成对人类偏好数据发现策略级准则集；(ii) 从结构充分性、可靠性、偏好拟合和对抗鲁棒性四个维度审计准则集。提出两种修复操作：偏好排名选择和可靠性约束精炼。

Result: 无原始准则源同时具备可靠、偏好预测和对抗鲁棒性；高评分者间一致性并不保证低可利用性。PReMISE是唯一在适用性、特异性和有效维度上同时表现非平凡的准则源。偏好排名选择将评判准确率从65.0%提升至68.6%；可靠性约束精炼将对抗利用比率从46.4%降至36.0%，同时评分者间一致性基本保持不变。

Conclusion: PReMISE框架能够系统性地发现和审计评估准则，在多个维度上优于现有准则源，并通过修复操作有效提升LLM评判器的可靠性和对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate open-ended responses, but their scores depend strongly on the rubrics that condition them. A vague rubric asking for a response to be ``helpful and factual'' can reward polished answers that invent facts or violate user intent. We treat reusable rubrics as measurement specifications: changing the rubric changes the response quality measurement induced by a fixed judge. We introduce PReMISE, a framework that, given pairwise human-preference data, (i) discovers a policy-level rubric set, and (ii) audits any rubric set under LLM-judge use along four axes: structural adequacy, reliability, preference fit, and adversarial robustness. Across rubric sources no raw source is simultaneously reliable, preference-predictive, and adversarially robust; and high inter-rater agreement does not imply low exploitability. PReMISE is the only rubric source to score non-trivially on applicability, specificity, and effective dimensionality simultaneously. We contribute two audit-targeted repair operations: preference-rank selection raises judge accuracy on paired responses from $65.0\%$ to $68.6\%$, competitive with the strongest rubric-discovery baselines and leading on two of three judges in our cross-judge sweep; reliability-constrained refinement reduces the rate at which exploit responses receive high scores from $46.4\%$ to $36.0\%$ with little change in inter-judge agreement ($α{=}.531\to.519$).

</details>


### [223] [SLAT: Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming for Efficient CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832)
*Jian Yao,Xiongcai Luo,Ran Cheng,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAT框架，通过段级自适应修剪解决大推理模型中的过度思考问题，在保持准确率的同时将推理长度减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大推理模型通过RL生成的CoT推理链存在结构性冗余（过度思考），导致高计算开销但不提高答案正确性。已有的长度惩罚方法过于粗糙，无法区分有用推理与冗余段。

Method: 提出SLAT（Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming）框架，基于正确性-长度权衡目标对段级次优性进行理论刻画，通过RL选择性抑制高概率但低边际效用的冗余段。

Result: 在标准基准上，SLAT建立了更优的准确率-效率帕累托前沿，相比未压缩基线减少50%推理长度，同时保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 理论驱动的段感知修剪是大语言模型实现高效CoT推理的有前景方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models have significantly improved chain-of-thought (CoT) capabilities via reinforcement learning (RL). However, generated reasoning chains frequently suffer from structural redundancy (i.e., \emph{overthinking}), incurring high computational overhead without improving answer correctness. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on token-uniform length penalties, which provide coarse, segment-agnostic pressure toward shorter outputs and can inadvertently suppress useful reasoning alongside redundancy. To address this, we demonstrate that inefficiency concentrates in high-probability segments with low marginal utility. We derive a theoretical characterization of segment suboptimality under the correctness-length trade-off objective and propose \textsc{SLAT} (Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming), an RL framework that selectively suppresses redundant segments based on this criterion. Empirical results on standard benchmarks indicate that \textsc{SLAT} establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier, reducing reasoning length by $50\%$ relative to uncompressed baselines while maintaining competitive accuracy. Overall, our results suggest that theoretically grounded, segment-aware trimming is a promising direction for efficient CoT reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [224] [Distilling LLM Feedback for Lean Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30861)
*Gaetan Narozniak,Gérard Biau,Rémi Munos,Ahmad Rammal,Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈蒸馏（Feedback Distillation），一种让模型在token级别匹配自身在特权反馈（由语言模型生成）条件下的分布的训练方法。在Lean4定理证明任务中，该方法相比GRPO保持更高的轨迹多样性（更高的策略熵和更好的pass@k扩展性），且两种方法互补：用反馈蒸馏检查点初始化GRPO优于单独使用任一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO（以及一般的强化学习）在推理模型的后训练中存在奖励稀疏、探索受限和模式坍缩问题，亟需一种能提供更密集监督信号并维持多样性的训练方法。

Method: 提出Feedback Distillation：训练模型在token级别匹配其自身在特权反馈（由语言模型生成的关于正确性等信息）条件下的分布，本质上是基于自蒸馏（self-distillation）的范式，通过语言模型提供的特权信息来注入外部知识并提供细粒度监督。

Result: 在Lean4定理证明任务上，Feedback Distillation相比GRPO产生更高的策略熵和更好的pass@k扩展性；用Feedback Distillation检查点初始化GRPO优于单独使用任一方法，表明两者互补。

Conclusion: Feedback Distillation为复杂推理任务的后训练提供了一条有前景的改进路径，能够克服GRPO的稀疏奖励和模式坍缩问题，且可与GRPO结合使用获得进一步提升。

Abstract: Post-training for reasoning models typically combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, most commonly with GRPO. However, this algorithm suffers from sparse rewards, limited exploration, and mode collapse. Building upon recent works on self-distillation, we propose Feedback Distillation, a training method where the model is trained to match, at the token level, its own distribution conditioned on privileged feedback produced by a language model. Feedback Distillation offers token-level supervision and can inject external knowledge. Evaluating our method for Lean4 theorem-proving, we find that Feedback Distillation maintains greater diversity in generated trajectories than GRPO, yielding higher policy entropy and better pass@k scaling. The two methods are complementary: initializing GRPO from a Feedback Distillation checkpoint outperforms either method alone. All in all, our results suggest a promising avenue to improve post-training for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [225] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang,Xingyu Wang,Mingze Kong,Zhubo Shi,Yuqian Hou,Hong Xu,Zhongxiang Dai,Minchen Yu,Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一推理缩放（UIS）框架，将模型路由与测试时缩放（TTS）统一到一个优化空间中，并设计了UniScale在线学习系统，通过上下文多臂赌博机和LinUCB算法实现细粒度、动态的推理质量-成本权衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由（在不同规模模型间切换）与测试时缩放（在固定模型内调整推理计算量）视为两个独立维度，导致以下问题：模型路由因可用模型规模稀疏而产生粗粒度、离散的性能变化；单模型TTS存在能力天花板且随着计算量增加出现收益递减；两者分离限制了动态推理环境下的适应性。

Method: 提出Unified Inference Scaling (UIS)，将模型路由和TTS统一到单一优化空间中。基于此，设计UniScale在线框架，将自适应UIS建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，使用LinUCB算法学习推理策略，并引入效率感知学习和成本建模以在高效动作空间上实现稳定可扩展的优化。

Result: 实验表明，UniScale有效利用了UIS空间中的协同效应，在各种动态推理场景中实现了细粒度且持续更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 统一模型路由与测试时缩放能够克服各自独立时的局限性，通过在线学习框架实现更灵活、更高效的推理决策。

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [226] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang,Xiaogang Li,Ruochen Gao,Peiyao Xiao,Chengliang Xu,Zeyu Wang,Zichao Chen,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BilliardPhys-Bench 基准测试，用于评估多模态大模型在台球物理场景中的直观物理推理能力，发现模型存在“静态偏差”（stasis bias）：当物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现良好，但在直观物理推理（如从单张图像预测物体运动与交互）方面仍然薄弱，缺乏相应的评估基准。

Method: 构建 BilliardPhys-Bench 基准，使用过程化引擎生成带有摩擦和弹性碰撞的随机台球场景，测试模型在三种物理推理能力上的表现：球-球碰撞预测、墙反弹推理、运动停止后最终球位估计。

Result: 评估了 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 系列的最新 MLLM，发现性能随模拟时间增加和场景几何复杂度上升而下降，并观察到一致的“静态偏差”失败模式。

Conclusion: 当前 MLLM 在视觉动力学推理上存在明显短板，需要在多模态架构中引入更好的物理归纳偏置来提升直观物理推理能力。

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [227] [A Persona-Based Evaluation Framework for Pluralistic Alignment in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31021)
*Atahan Karagoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态空间约束的AI评估框架，利用合成认知画像模拟多元人类视角，但发现这些模拟评估者在连续推理中存在一致性退化（状态空间漂移和语义不一致），进而论证需在生成系统中嵌入动态可适应性调节机制以维持稳健的评估行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐评估依赖单一基准框架，将人类判断的多元性简化为聚合统计基线，忽视了文化、人口统计和语境差异，无法反映真实世界中的共识变异性。

Method: 构建状态空间约束的仿真框架，用合成认知画像（synthetic cognitive profiles）替代单一评估函数，形成多样化人类视角的结构化流形；通过生成式架构实例化并维持这些评估者角色，并在顺序推理和随机提示扰动下分析其稳定性。

Result: 生成式架构能以较高一致性实例化评估者角色，但在顺序推理和随机扰动下出现系统性退化，表现为状态空间漂移（state-space drift）和语义不一致（semantic inconsistency）。

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持长期稳健的评估行为，需在生成系统中嵌入动态、可适应性调节机制；将基于角色的评估建模为潜表示流形上的结构化动力系统，为实现更具适应性、与人类对齐且上下文敏感的AI评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability.
